    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ReviewOptions:
    25→    """All user-facing review command options extracted once from argparse."""
    26→
    27→    merge: bool = False
    28→    run_batches: bool = False
    29→    import_run_dir: str | None = None
    30→    external_start: bool = False
    31→    external_submit: bool = False
    32→    import_file: str | None = None
    33→    validate_import_file: str | None = None
    34→    session_id: str | None = None
    35→    allow_partial: bool = False

    38→    dry_run: bool = False

    41→    attest: str | None = None
    42→
    43→    @classmethod
    44→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ReviewOptions:
    45→        return cls(
    46→            merge=bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)),
    47→            run_batches=bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)),
    48→            import_run_dir=getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None),
    49→            external_start=bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)),
    50→            external_submit=bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)),
    51→            import_file=getattr(args, "import_file", None),
    52→            validate_import_file=getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None),
    53→            session_id=getattr(args, "session_id", None),
    54→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
    55→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
    56→            path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    57→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),

    60→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),

    62→
    63→
    64→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    65→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    66→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    67→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    68→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    69→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    70→            continue
    71→        try:
    72→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    73→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    74→            _ = exc
    75→
    76→
    77→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    78→    if lang:
    79→        return
    80→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _mode_flags(opts: ReviewOptions) -> list[bool]:
    84→    import_mode = bool(opts.import_file) and not opts.external_submit
    85→    return [
    86→        opts.merge,
    87→        opts.run_batches,

    90→        opts.external_submit,
    91→        import_mode,
    92→        bool(opts.validate_import_file),
    93→    ]
    94→
    95→
    96→def _validate_mode_selection(
    97→    opts: ReviewOptions,
    98→    *,
    99→    mode_flags: list[bool],
   100→) -> None:
   101→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
   102→        raise CommandError(
   103→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
   104→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
   105→            exit_code=1,
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.import_file:
   109→        raise CommandError(
   110→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
   111→            exit_code=2,
   112→        )
   113→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.session_id:

   119→
   120→def _is_default_prepare_mode(opts: ReviewOptions, mode_flags: list[bool]) -> bool:
   121→    """True when the command will run the default prepare flow (no explicit mode)."""
   122→    return not any(mode_flags) and not opts.import_file
   123→
   124→
   125→def _run_review_mode(
   126→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   127→    *,
   128→    opts: ReviewOptions,
   129→    runtime,
   130→    state,
   131→    lang,
   132→    state_file,
   133→) -> None:
   134→    if opts.merge:
   135→        do_merge(args)
   136→        return
   137→    if opts.run_batches:
   138→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   139→        do_run_batches(
   140→            args,
   141→            state,
   142→            lang,
   143→            state_file,
   144→            config=runtime.config,
   145→        )
   146→        return
   147→    if opts.import_run_dir:
   148→        do_import_run(
   149→            opts.import_run_dir,
   150→            state,
   151→            lang,
   152→            state_file,
   153→            config=runtime.config,
   154→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   155→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   156→            scan_path=opts.path,
   157→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   158→        )
   159→        return
   160→    if opts.external_start:
   161→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   162→        do_external_start(
   163→            args,
   164→            state,
   165→            lang,
   166→            config=runtime.config,
   167→        )
   168→        return
   169→    if opts.external_submit:
   170→        do_external_submit(
   171→            import_file=str(opts.import_file),
   172→            session_id=str(opts.session_id),
   173→            state=state,
   174→            lang=lang,
   175→            state_file=state_file,
   176→            config=runtime.config,
   177→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   178→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   179→            scan_path=opts.path,
   180→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   181→        )
   182→        return
   183→    if opts.validate_import_file:
   184→        do_validate_import(
   185→            opts.validate_import_file,
   186→            lang,
   187→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   188→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   189→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   190→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   191→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   192→            ),
   193→        )
   194→        return
   195→
   196→    if opts.import_file:
   197→        do_import(
   198→            opts.import_file,
   199→            state,
   200→            lang,
   201→            state_file,
   202→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   203→                config=runtime.config,
   204→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   205→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   206→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   207→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   208→            ),
   209→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   210→        )
   211→        return
   212→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   213→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   214→
   215→
   216→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   217→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   218→    _enable_live_review_output()
   219→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   220→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   221→    state = runtime.state
   222→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   223→    _require_lang(lang)
   224→
   225→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   226→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   227→    _validate_mode_selection(
   228→        opts,
   229→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # Default prepare and --run-batches need real scan-backed context rather
   233→    # than reconstructed plan inventory.
   234→    if _is_default_prepare_mode(opts, mode_flags) or opts.run_batches:
   235→        if not require_scan_metrics(state):
   236→            return
   237→
   238→    _run_review_mode(
   239→        args,
   240→        opts=opts,
   241→        runtime=runtime,
   242→        state=state,
   243→        lang=lang,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→    )
   246→
```

   101→def _batch_live_log_interval_seconds(heartbeat_seconds: float) -> float:

   105→    return max(1.0, min(heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))

   201→def _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, *, config: dict | None) -> dict[str, object]:
   202→    """Build normalized invocation contract for prepared packet reuse."""
   203→    return prepared_packet_contract(resolve_review_packet_context(args), config=config)
   204→
   205→
   206→def _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   207→    packet: dict[str, object],
   208→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   209→) -> str | None:
   210→    """Return mismatch reason for prepared packet reuse, else ``None``."""
   211→    raw_contract = packet.get(_PREPARED_PACKET_CONTRACT_KEY)
   212→    if not isinstance(raw_contract, dict):
   213→        return "missing prepared packet contract metadata"
   214→
   215→    for key in (
   216→        "path",
   217→        "state_path",
   218→        "dimensions",
   219→        "retrospective",
   220→        "retrospective_max_issues",
   221→        "retrospective_max_batch_items",
   222→        "config_hash",
   223→    ):
   224→        if raw_contract.get(key) != expected_contract.get(key):
   225→            return f"contract field '{key}' differs"
   226→    return None
   227→
   228→
   229→def _try_load_prepared_packet(
   230→    *,
   231→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   232→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
   233→    """Load prepared packet from query.json when shape and contract match."""
   234→    try:
   235→        qf = query_file_path()
   236→        if not qf.exists():
   237→            return None, None
   238→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
   239→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, RuntimeError):
   240→        return None, "query.json is missing or invalid JSON"
   241→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   242→        return None, "prepared packet payload is not an object"
   243→    if "investigation_batches" not in data:
   244→        return None, "prepared packet is missing investigation batches"
   245→    batches = data["investigation_batches"]
   246→    if not isinstance(batches, list) or not batches:
   247→        return None, "prepared packet has no investigation batches"
   248→
   249→    mismatch_reason = _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   250→        data,
   251→        expected_contract,
   252→    )
   253→    if mismatch_reason is not None:
   254→        return None, mismatch_reason
   255→    return data, None
   256→
   257→
   258→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
   259→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
   260→    normalized_results: list[BatchResultPayload] = []
   261→    for result in batch_results:
   262→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
   263→            payload = result.to_dict()
   264→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
   265→                normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, payload))
   266→                continue
   267→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   268→            normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, result))
   269→    return merge_batch_results(
   270→        normalized_results,
   271→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   272→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   273→    )
   274→
   275→
   276→def _load_or_prepare_packet(
   277→    request: review_batches_mod.LoadOrPreparePacketRequest,
   278→) -> tuple[dict, Path, Path]:
   279→    """Load packet override or prepare a fresh packet snapshot."""
   280→    args = request.args
   281→    state = request.state
   282→    lang = request.lang
   283→    config = request.config
   284→    stamp = request.stamp
   285→    packet_override = getattr(args, "packet", None)
   286→    if packet_override:
   287→        packet_path = Path(packet_override)
   288→        if not packet_path.exists():
   289→            raise PacketValidationError(f"packet not found: {packet_override}", exit_code=1)
   290→        try:
   291→            packet = json.loads(packet_path.read_text())
   292→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   293→            raise PacketValidationError(f"reading packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   294→        blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   295→        blind_packet = build_blind_packet(packet)
   296→        safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   297→        print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   298→        print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_path}", "dim"))
   299→        return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   300→
   301→    # When no explicit --packet and no explicit --dimensions were given,
   302→    # check whether a prior ``review --prepare`` left a valid query.json
   303→    # packet we can reuse instead of rebuilding from scratch.
   304→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   305→
   306→    # Validate explicit dimensions against the language's scored dimensions.
   307→    if dims:
   308→        lang_obj = lang
   309→        lang_name = getattr(lang_obj, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang_obj, "lang", ""))
   310→        if lang_name:
   311→            valid_dims = set(scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name))
   312→            if valid_dims:
   313→                invalid = sorted(dims - valid_dims)
   314→                if invalid:
   315→                    valid_list = ", ".join(sorted(valid_dims))
   316→                    raise CommandError(
   317→                        f"Invalid dimensions for language '{lang_name}': {', '.join(invalid)}. "
   318→                        f"Valid dimensions: {valid_list}",
   319→                        exit_code=1,
   320→                    )
   321→
   322→    expected_contract = _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, config=config)
   323→    if not dims:
   324→        prepared, mismatch_reason = _try_load_prepared_packet(
   325→            expected_contract=expected_contract,
   326→        )
   327→        if prepared is not None:
   328→            print(colorize("  Reusing prepared packet from query.json", "dim"))
   329→            blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   330→            blind_packet = build_blind_packet(prepared)
   331→            safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   332→            packet_path, blind_saved = write_packet_snapshot(
   333→                prepared,
   334→                stamp=stamp,
   335→                review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   336→                blind_path=blind_path,
   337→                safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   338→            )
   339→            print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   340→            print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   341→            return prepared, packet_path, blind_saved
   342→        if mismatch_reason:
   343→            print(
   344→                colorize(
   345→                    f"  Prepared packet reuse rejected: {mismatch_reason}; rebuilding.",
   346→                    "dim",
   347→                )
   348→            )
   349→
   350→    context = resolve_review_packet_context(args)
   351→    blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   352→    packet, _lang_name = build_holistic_packet(
   353→        state=state,
   354→        lang=lang,
   355→        config=config,
   356→        context=context,
   357→        setup_lang_fn=_setup_lang,
   358→        prepare_holistic_review_fn=prepare_holistic_review,
   359→    )
   360→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
   361→    packet[_PREPARED_PACKET_CONTRACT_KEY] = expected_contract
   362→    packet["next_command"] = build_run_batches_next_command(context)
   363→    write_query_best_effort(
   364→        packet,
   365→        context="review packet query update",
   366→    )
   367→    packet_path, blind_saved = write_packet_snapshot(
   368→        packet,
   369→        stamp=stamp,
   370→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   371→        blind_path=blind_path,
   372→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   373→    )
   374→    print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   375→    print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   376→    return packet, packet_path, blind_saved

   378→
   379→def do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   380→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   381→    from ..runtime.policy import resolve_batch_run_policy  # noqa: PLC0415
   382→
   383→    project_root = _runtime_project_root()
   384→    subagent_runs_dir = _subagent_runs_dir()
   385→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   386→    batch_deps = _build_batch_run_deps(
   387→        policy=policy,
   388→        project_root=project_root,
   389→    )
   390→    prepared = review_batch_phases_mod.prepare_batch_run(
   391→        args=args,
   392→        state=state,
   393→        lang=lang,
   394→        config=config or {},
   395→        deps=batch_deps,
   396→        project_root=project_root,
   397→        subagent_runs_dir=subagent_runs_dir,
   398→    )
   399→    if prepared is None:
   400→        return
   401→
   402→    executed = review_batch_phases_mod.execute_batch_run(
   403→        prepared=prepared,
   404→        deps=batch_deps,
   405→    )
   406→    review_batch_phases_mod.merge_and_import_batch_run(
   407→        prepared=prepared,
   408→        executed=executed,
   409→        state_file=state_file,
   410→        deps=batch_deps,
   411→    )
   412→
   413→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   414→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   415→
   416→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   417→    """
   418→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   419→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   420→
   421→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   422→    if not summary_path.exists():
   423→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   424→    try:
   425→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   426→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   427→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   428→
   429→    selected = summary.get("selected_batches", [])
   430→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   431→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   432→
   433→    if not selected:
   434→        raise CommandError("no selected batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   435→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   436→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   437→
   438→    try:
   439→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   440→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   441→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   442→
   443→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   444→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   445→
   446→
   447→def do_import_run(
   448→    run_dir_path: str,
   449→    state: dict,
   450→    lang,
   451→    state_file: str,
   452→    *,
   453→    config: dict | None = None,
   454→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   455→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   456→    scan_path: str = ".",
   457→    dry_run: bool = False,
   458→) -> None:
   459→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   460→
   461→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   462→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   463→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   464→    """
   465→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   466→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   467→
   468→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   469→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   470→    selected = summary.get("selected_batches", [])
   471→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   472→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   473→
   474→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   475→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   476→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in selected]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   477→    output_files = {
   478→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   479→        for idx in selected_indexes
   480→    }
   481→
   482→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   483→    if missing:
   484→        if not results_dir.exists():
   485→            hint = (
   486→                f"Results directory does not exist: {results_dir}\n"
   487→                "  Did you run --run-batches or launch subagents to produce results first?"
   488→            )
   489→        elif len(missing) == len(selected):
   490→            hint = (
   491→                f"No result files found in {results_dir}\n"
   492→                "  Each subagent must write its output to results/batch-N.raw.txt.\n"
   493→                "  Run --run-batches first, or launch subagents on the prompts/ files manually."
   494→            )
   495→        else:
   496→            hint = (
   497→                f"Missing result files in {results_dir}: batches {missing}\n"
   498→                "  Re-run the failed batches or use --allow-partial to import what succeeded."
   499→            )
   500→        raise CommandError(hint, exit_code=1)
   501→
   502→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   503→        request=review_batches_mod.CollectBatchResultsRequest(
   504→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   505→            failures=[],
   506→            output_files=output_files,
   507→            allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   508→        ),
   509→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   510→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: normalize_batch_result(
   511→            payload,
   512→            dims,
   513→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   514→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   515→        ),
   516→    )
   517→
   518→    if not batch_results:
   519→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   520→
   521→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   522→    if failures:
   523→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   524→
   525→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   526→
   527→    # Reuse the canonical merge+metadata boundary from normal batch execution.
   528→    raw_batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
   529→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   530→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   531→        raw_batches if isinstance(raw_batches, list) else [],
   532→        dimension_prompts=raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else None,
   533→    )
   534→    packet_dimensions = normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   535→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   536→    scored_dimensions = scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name) if lang_name else []
   537→    merged_path, missing_after_import = _merge_and_write_results(
   538→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   539→        build_import_provenance_fn=build_batch_import_provenance,
   540→        batch_results=batch_results,
   541→        batches=batches,
   542→        successful_indexes=successful_indexes,
   543→        packet=packet,
   544→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   545→        scored_dimensions=scored_dimensions,
   546→        scan_path=scan_path,
   547→        runner=runner,
   548→        prompt_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   549→        stamp=stamp,
   550→        run_dir=run_dir,
   551→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   552→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   553→    )
   554→    _enforce_import_coverage(
   555→        missing_after_import=missing_after_import,
   556→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   557→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   558→        scan_path=scan_path,
   559→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   560→    )
   561→
   562→    # -- import with trusted source --
   563→    _do_import(
   564→        str(merged_path),
   565→        state,
   566→        lang,
   567→        state_file,
   568→        import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   569→            config=config,
   570→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   571→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   572→            trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   573→        ),
   574→        dry_run=dry_run,
   575→    )
   576→
   577→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   578→    if scan_after_import and not dry_run:
   579→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   580→        if lang_name:
   581→            run_followup_scan(
   582→                lang_name=lang_name,
   583→                scan_path=scan_path,
   584→                deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   585→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   586→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   587→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   588→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   589→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   590→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   591→                ),
   592→            )
   593→

     2→

     9→

    11→    handle_early_attempt_return,
    12→    handle_failed_attempt,
    13→    handle_successful_attempt,

    24→
    25→def _resolve_executable(name: str) -> list[str]:
    26→    """Resolve an executable, handling Windows .cmd/.bat wrappers.
    27→
    28→    On Windows, npm-installed CLIs are ``.cmd`` batch scripts that cannot be
    29→    executed directly by ``subprocess`` without ``shell=True``.  Prefixing
    30→    with ``cmd /c`` avoids needing ``shell=True`` while still resolving them.
    31→
    32→    When ``shutil.which()`` cannot locate the executable on Windows, we still
    33→    route through ``cmd /c`` so the shell's own PATH resolution can find
    34→    ``.cmd``/``.bat`` wrappers that Python's ``which`` missed.
    35→    """
    36→    resolved = shutil.which(name)
    37→    if sys.platform == "win32":
    38→        if resolved is not None and resolved.lower().endswith((".cmd", ".bat")):
    39→            return ["cmd", "/c", resolved]
    40→        # shutil.which may miss .cmd/.bat wrappers — let cmd.exe resolve it
    41→        return ["cmd", "/c", resolved or name]
    42→    return [resolved or name]
    43→
    44→
    45→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    46→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
    47→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
    48→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
    49→        effort = "low"
    50→    prefix = _resolve_executable("codex")
    51→    return [
    52→        *prefix,
    53→        "exec",
    54→        "--ephemeral",
    55→        "-C",
    56→        str(repo_root),
    57→        "-s",
    58→        "workspace-write",
    59→        "-c",
    60→        'approval_policy="never"',
    61→        "-c",
    62→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
    63→        "-o",
    64→        str(output_file),
    65→        prompt,
    66→    ]
    67→
    68→
    69→def run_codex_batch(
    70→    *,
    71→    prompt: str,
    72→    repo_root: Path,
    73→    output_file: Path,
    74→    log_file: Path,
    75→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    76→    codex_batch_command_fn=None,
    77→) -> int:
    78→    """Execute one codex batch and return a stable CLI-style status code."""
    79→    if codex_batch_command_fn is None:
    80→        codex_batch_command_fn = codex_batch_command
    81→    cmd = codex_batch_command_fn(
    82→        prompt=prompt,
    83→        repo_root=repo_root,
    84→        output_file=output_file,
    85→    )
    86→    config = resolve_retry_config(deps)
    87→    log_sections: list[str] = []
    88→
    89→    for attempt in range(1, config.max_attempts + 1):
    90→        header, result = run_batch_attempt(
    91→            cmd=cmd,
    92→            deps=deps,
    93→            output_file=output_file,
    94→            log_file=log_file,
    95→            log_sections=log_sections,
    96→            attempt=attempt,
    97→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
    98→            use_popen=config.use_popen,
    99→            live_log_interval=config.live_log_interval,
   100→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
   101→        )
   102→        early_return = handle_early_attempt_return(result)
   103→        if early_return is not None:
   104→            return early_return
   105→        timeout_or_stall = handle_timeout_or_stall(
   106→            header=header,
   107→            result=result,
   108→            deps=deps,
   109→            output_file=output_file,
   110→            log_file=log_file,
   111→            log_sections=log_sections,
   112→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
   113→        )
   114→        if timeout_or_stall is not None:
   115→            if timeout_or_stall == 0:
   116→                return 0  # recovered from timeout/stall
   117→            # Non-recovered timeout/stall: retry if attempts remain
   118→            if attempt < config.max_attempts:
   119→                delay = config.retry_backoff_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))
   120→                log_sections.append(
   121→                    f"Timeout/stall on attempt {attempt}/{config.max_attempts}; "
   122→                    f"retrying in {delay:.1f}s."
   123→                )
   124→                if delay > 0:
   125→                    deps.sleep_fn(delay)
   126→                continue
   127→            return timeout_or_stall
   128→
   129→        log_sections.append(
   130→            f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
   131→        )
   132→
   133→        success_code = handle_successful_attempt(
   134→            result=result,
   135→            output_file=output_file,
   136→            log_file=log_file,
   137→            deps=deps,
   138→            log_sections=log_sections,
   139→        )
   140→        if success_code is not None:
   141→            return success_code
   142→        failure_code = handle_failed_attempt(
   143→            result=result,
   144→            deps=deps,
   145→            attempt=attempt,
   146→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
   147→            retry_backoff_seconds=config.retry_backoff_seconds,
   148→            log_file=log_file,
   149→            log_sections=log_sections,
   150→        )
   151→        if failure_code is not None:
   152→            return failure_code
   153→
   154→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   155→    return 1
   156→
   157→
   158→def run_followup_scan(
   159→    *,
   160→    lang_name: str,
   161→    scan_path: str,
   162→    deps: FollowupScanDeps,
   163→    force_queue_bypass: bool = False,
   164→) -> int:
   165→    """Run a follow-up scan and return a non-zero status when it fails."""
   166→    scan_cmd = [
   167→        deps.python_executable,
   168→        "-m",
   169→        "desloppify",
   170→        "--lang",
   171→        lang_name,
   172→        "scan",
   173→        "--path",
   174→        scan_path,
   175→    ]
   176→    if force_queue_bypass:
   177→        followup_attest = (
   178→            "I understand this is not the intended workflow and "

   191→            scan_cmd,

   195→    except deps.timeout_error:

   199→                "yellow",

   201→            file=sys.stderr,
   202→        )
   203→        return 124
   204→    except OSError as exc:
   205→        print(
   206→            deps.colorize_fn(f"  Follow-up scan failed: {exc}", "red"),
   207→            file=sys.stderr,
   208→        )
   209→        return 1
   210→    return int(getattr(result, "returncode", 0) or 0)

1→"""Attempt execution and retry orchestration for review batch runner."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import subprocess  # nosec
     6→import threading
     7→import time
     8→from contextlib import contextmanager
     9→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_failures import (
    13→    TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES as _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES,
    14→)
    15→
    16→from .attempt_success import handle_successful_attempt_core
    17→from .io import (
    18→    _check_stall,
    19→    _drain_stream,
    20→    _output_file_has_json_payload,
    21→    _start_live_writer,
    22→    _terminate_process,
    23→    _write_live_snapshot,
    24→)
    25→from .types import (
    26→    CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    27→    _AttemptContext,
    28→    _ExecutionResult,
    29→    _RetryConfig,
    30→    _RunnerState,
    31→)
    32→
    33→
    34→@contextmanager
    35→def _managed_live_writer(
    36→    state: _RunnerState,
    37→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
    38→    interval: float,
    39→):
    40→    """Start/stop the live-writer thread around one runner attempt."""
    41→    writer_thread = _start_live_writer(state, ctx, interval)
    42→    try:
    43→        yield
    44→    finally:
    45→        state.stop_event.set()
    46→        writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _runner_error_result(
    50→    *,
    51→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
    52→    heading: str,
    53→    exc: Exception,
    54→    exit_code: int,
    55→) -> _ExecutionResult:
    56→    """Build a consistent error result for runner invocation failures."""
    57→    ctx.log_sections.append(f"{ctx.header}\n\n{heading}:\n{exc}\n")
    58→    ctx.safe_write_text_fn(ctx.log_file, "\n\n".join(ctx.log_sections))
    59→    return _ExecutionResult(
    60→        code=exit_code,
    61→        stdout_text="",
    62→        stderr_text="",
    63→        early_return=exit_code,
    64→    )
    65→
    66→
    67→def _run_via_popen(
    68→    cmd: list[str],
    69→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    70→    state: _RunnerState,
    71→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
    72→    interval: float,
    73→    stall_seconds: int,
    74→) -> _ExecutionResult:
    75→    with _managed_live_writer(state, ctx, interval):
    76→        process_or_error = _start_runner_process(cmd, deps, ctx)
    77→        if isinstance(process_or_error, _ExecutionResult):
    78→            return process_or_error
    79→        process = process_or_error
    80→        stdout_thread, stderr_thread = _start_stream_threads(process, state)
    81→        timed_out, stalled, recovered_from_stall = _monitor_runner_process(
    82→            process,
    83→            deps=deps,
    84→            state=state,
    85→            ctx=ctx,
    86→            interval=interval,
    87→            stall_seconds=stall_seconds,
    88→        )
    89→        _finalize_runner_process(process, stdout_thread, stderr_thread)
    90→        _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
    91→        return _ExecutionResult(
    92→            code=int(process.returncode or 0),
    93→            stdout_text="".join(state.stdout_chunks),
    94→            stderr_text="".join(state.stderr_chunks),
    95→            timed_out=timed_out,
    96→            stalled=stalled,
    97→            recovered_from_stall=recovered_from_stall,
    98→        )
    99→
   100→
   101→def _start_runner_process(
   102→    cmd: list[str],
   103→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   104→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   105→) -> subprocess.Popen[str] | _ExecutionResult:
   106→    try:
   107→        return deps.subprocess_popen(
   108→            cmd,
   109→            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   110→            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   111→            text=True,
   112→            bufsize=1,
   113→        )
   114→    except OSError as exc:
   115→        return _runner_error_result(
   116→            ctx=ctx,
   117→            heading="RUNNER ERROR",
   118→            exc=exc,
   119→            exit_code=127,
   120→        )
   121→    except (
   122→        RuntimeError,
   123→        ValueError,
   124→        TypeError,
   125→        subprocess.SubprocessError,
   126→    ) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   127→        return _runner_error_result(
   128→            ctx=ctx,
   129→            heading="UNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR",
   130→            exc=exc,
   131→            exit_code=1,
   132→        )
   133→
   134→
   135→def _start_stream_threads(
   136→    process: subprocess.Popen[str],
   137→    state: _RunnerState,
   138→) -> tuple[threading.Thread, threading.Thread]:
   139→    stdout_thread = threading.Thread(
   140→        target=_drain_stream,
   141→        args=(process.stdout, state.stdout_chunks, state),
   142→        daemon=True,
   143→    )
   144→    stderr_thread = threading.Thread(
   145→        target=_drain_stream,
   146→        args=(process.stderr, state.stderr_chunks, state),
   147→        daemon=True,
   148→    )
   149→    stdout_thread.start()
   150→    stderr_thread.start()
   151→    return stdout_thread, stderr_thread
   152→
   153→
   154→def _timed_out_runner_attempt(
   155→    process: subprocess.Popen[str],
   156→    *,
   157→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   158→    state: _RunnerState,
   159→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   160→) -> bool:
   161→    elapsed = int(max(0.0, time.monotonic() - ctx.started_monotonic))
   162→    if elapsed < deps.timeout_seconds:
   163→        return False
   164→    with state.lock:
   165→        state.runner_note = f"timeout after {deps.timeout_seconds}s"
   166→    _terminate_process(process)
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→def _check_runner_stall(
   171→    process: subprocess.Popen[str],
   172→    *,
   173→    state: _RunnerState,
   174→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   175→    stall_seconds: int,
   176→    output_signature: tuple[int, int] | None,
   177→    output_stable_since: float | None,
   178→) -> tuple[bool, bool, tuple[int, int] | None, float | None]:
   179→    with state.lock:
   180→        last_activity = state.last_stream_activity
   181→    stalled, output_signature, output_stable_since = _check_stall(
   182→        ctx.output_file,
   183→        output_signature,
   184→        output_stable_since,
   185→        time.monotonic(),
   186→        last_activity,
   187→        stall_seconds,
   188→    )
   189→    if not stalled:
   190→        return False, False, output_signature, output_stable_since
   191→    with state.lock:
   192→        state.runner_note = (
   193→            f"stall recovery triggered after {stall_seconds}s "
   194→            "with stable output state"
   195→        )
   196→    recovered_from_stall = _output_file_has_json_payload(ctx.output_file)
   197→    _terminate_process(process)
   198→    return True, recovered_from_stall, output_signature, output_stable_since
   199→
   200→
   201→def _monitor_runner_process(
   202→    process: subprocess.Popen[str],
   203→    *,
   204→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   205→    state: _RunnerState,
   206→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   207→    interval: float,
   208→    stall_seconds: int,
   209→) -> tuple[bool, bool, bool]:
   210→    timed_out = False
   211→    stalled = False
   212→    recovered_from_stall = False
   213→    output_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = None
   214→    output_stable_since: float | None = None
   215→
   216→    while process.poll() is None:
   217→        if _timed_out_runner_attempt(process, deps=deps, state=state, ctx=ctx):
   218→            timed_out = True
   219→            break
   220→        if stall_seconds > 0:
   221→            (
   222→                stalled,
   223→                recovered_from_stall,
   224→                output_signature,
   225→                output_stable_since,
   226→            ) = _check_runner_stall(
   227→                process,
   228→                state=state,
   229→                ctx=ctx,
   230→                stall_seconds=stall_seconds,
   231→                output_signature=output_signature,
   232→                output_stable_since=output_stable_since,
   233→            )
   234→            if stalled:
   235→                break
   236→        deps.sleep_fn(min(interval, 1.0))
   237→    return timed_out, stalled, recovered_from_stall
   238→
   239→
   240→def _finalize_runner_process(
   241→    process: subprocess.Popen[str],
   242→    stdout_thread: threading.Thread,
   243→    stderr_thread: threading.Thread,
   244→) -> None:
   245→    if process.poll() is None:
   246→        _terminate_process(process)
   247→    stdout_thread.join(timeout=2)
   248→    stderr_thread.join(timeout=2)
   249→
   250→
   251→def _run_via_subprocess(
   252→    cmd: list[str],
   253→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   254→    state: _RunnerState,
   255→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   256→    interval: float,
   257→) -> _ExecutionResult:
   258→    with _managed_live_writer(state, ctx, interval):
   259→        try:
   260→            result = deps.subprocess_run(
   261→                cmd,
   262→                capture_output=True,
   263→                text=True,
   264→                timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   265→            )
   266→        except deps.timeout_error:
   267→            return _ExecutionResult(code=124, stdout_text="", stderr_text="", timed_out=True)
   268→        except OSError as exc:
   269→            return _runner_error_result(
   270→                ctx=ctx,
   271→                heading="RUNNER ERROR",
   272→                exc=exc,
   273→                exit_code=127,
   274→            )
   275→        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   276→            return _runner_error_result(
   277→                ctx=ctx,
   278→                heading="UNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR",
   279→                exc=exc,
   280→                exit_code=1,
   281→            )
   282→
   283→        return _ExecutionResult(
   284→            code=int(result.returncode),
   285→            stdout_text=result.stdout or "",
   286→            stderr_text=result.stderr or "",
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→def resolve_retry_config(deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps) -> _RetryConfig:
   291→    retries_raw = deps.max_retries if isinstance(deps.max_retries, int) else 0
   292→    max_retries = max(0, retries_raw)
   293→    max_attempts = max_retries + 1
   294→    backoff_raw = (
   295→        float(deps.retry_backoff_seconds)
   296→        if isinstance(deps.retry_backoff_seconds, int | float)
   297→        else 0.0
   298→    )
   299→    retry_backoff_seconds = max(0.0, backoff_raw)
   300→    live_log_interval = (
   301→        float(deps.live_log_interval_seconds)
   302→        if isinstance(deps.live_log_interval_seconds, int | float)
   303→        and float(deps.live_log_interval_seconds) > 0
   304→        else 5.0
   305→    )
   306→    stall_seconds = (
   307→        int(deps.stall_after_output_seconds)
   308→        if isinstance(deps.stall_after_output_seconds, int | float)
   309→        and int(deps.stall_after_output_seconds) > 0
   310→        else 0
   311→    )
   312→    use_popen = bool(deps.use_popen_runner) and callable(
   313→        getattr(deps, "subprocess_popen", None)
   314→    )
   315→    return _RetryConfig(
   316→        max_attempts=max_attempts,
   317→        retry_backoff_seconds=retry_backoff_seconds,
   318→        live_log_interval=live_log_interval,
   319→        stall_seconds=stall_seconds,
   320→        use_popen=use_popen,
   321→    )
   322→
   323→
   324→def run_batch_attempt(
   325→    *,
   326→    cmd: list[str],
   327→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   328→    output_file: Path,
   329→    log_file: Path,
   330→    log_sections: list[str],
   331→    attempt: int,
   332→    max_attempts: int,
   333→    use_popen: bool,
   334→    live_log_interval: float,
   335→    stall_seconds: int,
   336→) -> tuple[str, _ExecutionResult]:
   337→    header = f"ATTEMPT {attempt}/{max_attempts}\n$ {' '.join(cmd)}"
   338→    started_monotonic = time.monotonic()
   339→    state = _RunnerState(last_stream_activity=started_monotonic)
   340→    ctx = _AttemptContext(
   341→        header=header,
   342→        started_at_iso=datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   343→        started_monotonic=started_monotonic,
   344→        output_file=output_file,
   345→        log_file=log_file,
   346→        log_sections=log_sections,
   347→        safe_write_text_fn=deps.safe_write_text_fn,
   348→    )
   349→    _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
   350→    if use_popen:
   351→        result = _run_via_popen(
   352→            cmd,
   353→            deps,
   354→            state,
   355→            ctx,
   356→            live_log_interval,
   357→            stall_seconds,
   358→        )
   359→    else:
   360→        result = _run_via_subprocess(cmd, deps, state, ctx, live_log_interval)
   361→    return header, result
   362→
   363→
   364→def handle_early_attempt_return(result: _ExecutionResult) -> int | None:
   365→    return result.early_return
   366→
   367→
   368→def handle_timeout_or_stall(
   369→    *,
   370→    header: str,
   371→    result: _ExecutionResult,
   372→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   373→    output_file: Path,
   374→    log_file: Path,
   375→    log_sections: list[str],
   376→    stall_seconds: int,
   377→) -> int | None:
   378→    if not result.timed_out and not result.stalled:
   379→        return None
   380→    if result.timed_out:
   381→        log_sections.append(
   382→            f"{header}\n\nTIMEOUT after {deps.timeout_seconds}s\n\n"
   383→            f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
   384→        )
   385→    else:
   386→        log_sections.append(
   387→            f"{header}\n\nSTALL RECOVERY after {stall_seconds}s "
   388→            "of stable output and no stream activity.\n\n"
   389→            f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
   390→        )
   391→    if _output_file_has_json_payload(output_file):
   392→        recovery_message = (
   393→            "Recovered timed-out batch from JSON output file; "
   394→            "continuing as success."
   395→            if result.timed_out
   396→            else "Recovered stalled batch from JSON output file; "
   397→            "continuing as success."
   398→        )
   399→        log_sections.append(recovery_message)
   400→        deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   401→        return 0
   402→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   403→    return 124
   404→
   405→
   406→def handle_successful_attempt(
   407→    *,
   408→    result: _ExecutionResult,
   409→    output_file: Path,
   410→    log_file: Path,
   411→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   412→    log_sections: list[str],
   413→) -> int | None:
   414→    if result.code != 0:
   415→        return None
   416→    if not output_file.exists():
   417→        log_sections.append("Runner returned 0 but output file is missing.")
   418→    validate_fn = _resolved_validate_output_fn(deps)
   419→    return handle_successful_attempt_core(
   420→        result=result,
   421→        output_file=output_file,
   422→        log_file=log_file,
   423→        deps=deps,
   424→        log_sections=log_sections,
   425→        default_validate_fn=validate_fn,
   426→        monotonic_fn=time.monotonic,
   427→    )
   428→
   429→
   430→def _resolved_validate_output_fn(deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps):
   431→    if deps.validate_output_fn is not None:
   432→        return deps.validate_output_fn
   433→    return _output_file_has_json_payload
   434→
   435→
   436→def handle_failed_attempt(
   437→    *,
   438→    result: _ExecutionResult,
   439→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   440→    attempt: int,
   441→    max_attempts: int,
   442→    retry_backoff_seconds: float,
   443→    log_file: Path,
   444→    log_sections: list[str],
   445→) -> int | None:
   446→    combined = f"{result.stdout_text}\n{result.stderr_text}".lower()
   447→    is_transient = any(needle in combined for needle in _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES)
   448→    if not is_transient or attempt >= max_attempts:
   449→        deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   450→        return result.code
   451→    delay_seconds = _retry_delay_seconds(
   452→        retry_backoff_seconds,
   453→        attempt=attempt,
   454→    )
   455→    log_sections.append(
   456→        "Transient runner failure detected; "
   457→        f"retrying in {delay_seconds:.1f}s (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_attempts})."
   458→    )
   459→    try:
   460→        if delay_seconds > 0:
   461→            deps.sleep_fn(delay_seconds)
   462→    except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   463→        log_sections.append(
   464→            f"Retry delay hook failed: {exc} — aborting remaining retries."
   465→        )
   466→        deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   467→        return 1
   468→    return None
   469→
   470→
   471→def _retry_delay_seconds(
   472→    retry_backoff_seconds: float,
   473→    *,
   474→    attempt: int,
   475→) -> float:
   476→    return retry_backoff_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))
   477→
   478→
   479→__all__ = [
   480→    "handle_early_attempt_return",
   481→    "handle_failed_attempt",
   482→    "handle_successful_attempt",
   483→    "handle_timeout_or_stall",
   484→    "resolve_retry_config",
   485→    "run_batch_attempt",
   486→    "_run_via_popen",
   487→    "_run_via_subprocess",
   488→]
   489→
```

   283→    stall_warned_batches: set[int] = set()
   284→    report_progress = build_progress_reporter(
   285→        batch_positions=batch_positions,
   286→        batch_status=batch_status,
   287→        stall_warned_batches=stall_warned_batches,
   288→        total_batches=total_batches,
   289→        stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   290→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   291→        output_files=output_files,
   292→        log_files=log_files,
   293→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   294→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   295→    )
   296→    record_issue = partial(record_execution_issue, append_run_log)
   297→    write_run_summary = build_run_summary_writer(
   298→        run_dir=run_dir,
   299→        summary_config=BatchRunSummaryConfig(
   300→            created_at=datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   301→            run_stamp=stamp,
   302→            runner=runner,
   303→            run_parallel=run_parallel,
   304→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   305→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   306→            max_parallel_batches=max_parallel_batches,
   307→            batch_timeout_seconds=batch_timeout_seconds,
   308→            batch_max_retries=batch_max_retries,
   309→            batch_retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   310→            heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   311→            stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   312→            stall_kill_seconds=stall_kill_seconds,
   313→            immutable_packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   314→            prompt_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   315→            run_dir=run_dir,
   316→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   317→            run_log_path=run_log_path,
   318→        ),
   319→        batch_status=batch_status,
   320→        safe_write_text_fn=deps.safe_write_text_fn,
   321→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   322→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   323→    )
   324→    return PreparedRunArtifacts(
   325→        run_dir=run_dir,
   326→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   327→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   328→        output_files=output_files,
   329→        log_files=log_files,
   330→        run_log_path=run_log_path,
   331→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   332→        batch_positions=batch_positions,
   333→        batch_status=batch_status,
   334→        report_progress=report_progress,
   335→        record_issue=record_issue,
   336→        write_run_summary=write_run_summary,
   337→    )
   338→
   339→
   340→def prepare_batch_run(
   341→    *,
   342→    args,
   343→    state,
   344→    lang,
   345→    config: dict[str, Any],
   346→    deps: BatchRunDeps,
   347→    project_root: Path,
   348→    subagent_runs_dir: Path,
   349→) -> PreparedBatchRunContext | None:
   350→    """Prepare packet/artifacts and return execution context; None for dry-run."""
   351→    runner = getattr(args, "runner", "codex")
   352→    validate_runner(runner, colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn)
   353→    allow_partial = bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False))
   354→
   355→    policy = _resolve_runtime_policy(args)
   356→
   357→    stamp = deps.run_stamp_fn()
   358→    packet_scope = _prepare_packet_scope(
   359→        args=args,
   360→        state=state,
   361→        lang=lang,
   362→        config=config,
   363→        deps=deps,
   364→        stamp=stamp,
   365→    )
   366→    total_batches = len(packet_scope.selected_indexes)
   367→    _print_runtime_expectation(
   368→        deps=deps,
   369→        total_batches=total_batches,
   370→        run_parallel=policy.run_parallel,
   371→        max_parallel_batches=policy.max_parallel_batches,
   372→        batch_timeout_seconds=policy.batch_timeout_seconds,
   373→    )
   374→    runtime_artifacts = _prepare_run_runtime(
   375→        args=args,
   376→        deps=deps,
   377→        stamp=stamp,
   378→        selected_indexes=packet_scope.selected_indexes,
   379→        batches=packet_scope.batches,
   380→        prompt_packet_path=packet_scope.prompt_packet_path,
   381→        immutable_packet_path=packet_scope.immutable_packet_path,
   382→        project_root=project_root,
   383→        subagent_runs_dir=subagent_runs_dir,
   384→        runner=runner,
   385→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   386→        run_parallel=policy.run_parallel,
   387→        max_parallel_batches=policy.max_parallel_batches,
   388→        heartbeat_seconds=policy.heartbeat_seconds,
   389→        batch_timeout_seconds=policy.batch_timeout_seconds,
   390→        batch_max_retries=policy.batch_max_retries,
   391→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   392→        stall_warning_seconds=policy.stall_warning_seconds,
   393→        stall_kill_seconds=policy.stall_kill_seconds,
   394→    )
   395→    if maybe_handle_dry_run(
   396→        args=args,
   397→        stamp=stamp,
   398→        selected_indexes=packet_scope.selected_indexes,
   399→        run_dir=runtime_artifacts.run_dir,
   400→        logs_dir=runtime_artifacts.logs_dir,
   401→        immutable_packet_path=packet_scope.immutable_packet_path,
   402→        prompt_packet_path=packet_scope.prompt_packet_path,
   403→        prompt_files=runtime_artifacts.prompt_files,
   404→        output_files=runtime_artifacts.output_files,
   405→        safe_write_text_fn=deps.safe_write_text_fn,
   406→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   407→        append_run_log=runtime_artifacts.append_run_log,
   408→    ):
   409→        return None
   410→
   411→    if policy.run_parallel:
   412→        print(
   413→            deps.colorize_fn(
   414→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   415→                f"max-workers={min(total_batches, policy.max_parallel_batches)}, "
   416→                f"heartbeat={policy.heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   417→                "dim",
   418→            )
   419→        )
   420→
   421→    return PreparedBatchRunContext(
   422→        stamp=stamp,
   423→        args=args,
   424→        config=config,
   425→        runner=runner,
   426→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   427→        run_parallel=policy.run_parallel,
   428→        max_parallel_batches=policy.max_parallel_batches,
   429→        heartbeat_seconds=policy.heartbeat_seconds,
   430→        batch_timeout_seconds=policy.batch_timeout_seconds,
   431→        batch_max_retries=policy.batch_max_retries,
   432→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   433→        stall_warning_seconds=policy.stall_warning_seconds,
   434→        stall_kill_seconds=policy.stall_kill_seconds,
   435→        state=state,
   436→        lang=lang,
   437→        packet=packet_scope.packet,
   438→        immutable_packet_path=packet_scope.immutable_packet_path,
   439→        prompt_packet_path=packet_scope.prompt_packet_path,
   440→        scan_path=packet_scope.scan_path,
   441→        packet_dimensions=packet_scope.packet_dimensions,
   442→        scored_dimensions=packet_scope.scored_dimensions,
   443→        batches=packet_scope.batches,
   444→        selected_indexes=packet_scope.selected_indexes,
   445→        project_root=project_root,
   446→        run_dir=runtime_artifacts.run_dir,
   447→        logs_dir=runtime_artifacts.logs_dir,
   448→        prompt_files=runtime_artifacts.prompt_files,
   449→        output_files=runtime_artifacts.output_files,
   450→        log_files=runtime_artifacts.log_files,
   451→        run_log_path=runtime_artifacts.run_log_path,
   452→        append_run_log=runtime_artifacts.append_run_log,
   453→        batch_positions=runtime_artifacts.batch_positions,
   454→        batch_status=runtime_artifacts.batch_status,
   455→        report_progress=runtime_artifacts.report_progress,
   456→        record_issue=runtime_artifacts.record_issue,
   457→        write_run_summary=runtime_artifacts.write_run_summary,
   458→    )
   459→
   460→
   461→def execute_batch_run(*, prepared: PreparedBatchRunContext, deps: BatchRunDeps) -> ExecutedBatchRunContext:
   462→    """Execute prepared tasks and return reconciliation outputs."""
   463→    selected_indexes = prepared.selected_indexes
   464→    tasks = build_batch_tasks(
   465→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   466→        prompt_files=prepared.prompt_files,
   467→        output_files=prepared.output_files,
   468→        log_files=prepared.log_files,
   469→        project_root=prepared.project_root,
   470→        run_codex_batch_fn=deps.run_codex_batch_fn,
   471→    )
   472→    try:
   473→        execution_failures = deps.execute_batches_fn(
   474→            tasks=tasks,
   475→            options=BatchExecutionOptions(
   476→                run_parallel=prepared.run_parallel,
   477→                max_parallel_workers=prepared.max_parallel_batches,
   478→                heartbeat_seconds=prepared.heartbeat_seconds,
   479→            ),
   480→            progress_fn=prepared.report_progress,
   481→            error_log_fn=prepared.record_issue,
   482→        )
   483→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   484→        mark_interrupted_batches(
   485→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   486→            batch_status=prepared.batch_status,
   487→            batch_positions=prepared.batch_positions,
   488→        )
   489→        prepared.write_run_summary(
   490→            successful_batches=[],
   491→            failed_batches=[],
   492→            interrupted=True,
   493→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   494→        )
   495→        prepared.append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   496→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   497→
   498→    batch_results, successful_indexes, failures, failure_set = collect_and_reconcile_results(
   499→        collect_batch_results_fn=deps.collect_batch_results_fn,
   500→        request=CollectBatchResultsRequest(
   501→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   502→            failures=execution_failures,
   503→            output_files=prepared.output_files,
   504→            allowed_dims={
   505→                str(dim)
   506→                for dim in prepared.packet.get("dimensions", [])
   507→                if isinstance(dim, str)
   508→            },
   509→        ),
   510→        execution_failures=execution_failures,
   511→        batch_positions=prepared.batch_positions,
   512→        batch_status=prepared.batch_status,
   513→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   514→    )
   515→    prepared.write_run_summary(
   516→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   517→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   518→    )
   519→
   520→    if failures and (not prepared.allow_partial or not batch_results):
   521→        prepared.append_run_log(
   522→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   523→        )
   524→        deps.print_failures_and_raise_fn(
   525→            failures=failures,
   526→            packet_path=prepared.immutable_packet_path,
   527→            logs_dir=prepared.logs_dir,
   528→            colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   529→        )
   530→    elif failures:
   531→        print(
   532→            deps.colorize_fn(
   533→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   534→                "yellow",
   535→            )
   536→        )
   537→        deps.print_failures_fn(
   538→            failures=failures,
   539→            packet_path=prepared.immutable_packet_path,
   540→            logs_dir=prepared.logs_dir,
   541→            colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   542→        )
   543→        prepared.append_run_log(
   544→            "run-partial "
   545→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   546→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   547→        )
   548→
   549→    return ExecutedBatchRunContext(
   550→        batch_results=batch_results,
   551→        successful_indexes=successful_indexes,
   552→        failure_set=failure_set,
   553→    )
   554→
   555→
   556→def merge_and_import_batch_run(
   557→    *,
   558→    prepared: PreparedBatchRunContext,
   559→    executed: ExecutedBatchRunContext,
   560→    state_file,
   561→    deps: BatchRunDeps,
   562→) -> None:
   563→    """Merge successful batch results and import final holistic output."""
   564→    merged_path, missing_after_import = merge_and_write_results(
   565→        merge_batch_results_fn=deps.merge_batch_results_fn,
   566→        build_import_provenance_fn=deps.build_import_provenance_fn,
   567→        batch_results=executed.batch_results,
   568→        batches=prepared.batches,
   569→        successful_indexes=executed.successful_indexes,
   570→        packet=prepared.packet,
   571→        packet_dimensions=prepared.packet_dimensions,
   572→        scored_dimensions=prepared.scored_dimensions,
   573→        scan_path=prepared.scan_path,
   574→        runner=prepared.runner,
   575→        prompt_packet_path=prepared.prompt_packet_path,
   576→        stamp=prepared.stamp,
   577→        run_dir=prepared.run_dir,
   578→        safe_write_text_fn=deps.safe_write_text_fn,
   579→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   580→    )
   581→    enforce_import_coverage(
   582→        missing_after_import=missing_after_import,
   583→        packet_dimensions=prepared.packet_dimensions,
   584→        allow_partial=prepared.allow_partial,
   585→        scan_path=prepared.scan_path,
   586→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   587→    )
   588→    import_and_finalize(
   589→        do_import_fn=deps.do_import_fn,
   590→        run_followup_scan_fn=deps.run_followup_scan_fn,
   591→        merged_path=merged_path,
   592→        state=prepared.state,
   593→        lang=prepared.lang,
   594→        state_file=state_file,
   595→        config=prepared.config,
   596→        allow_partial=prepared.allow_partial,
   597→        successful_indexes=executed.successful_indexes,
   598→        failure_set=executed.failure_set,
   599→        append_run_log=prepared.append_run_log,
   600→        args=prepared.args,
   601→    )
   602→
   603→
   604→__all__ = [
   605→    "ExecutedBatchRunContext",
   606→    "PreparedPacketScope",
   607→    "PreparedBatchRunContext",
   608→    "PreparedRunArtifacts",
   609→    "_prepare_packet_scope",
   610→    "_prepare_run_runtime",
   611→    "_print_runtime_expectation",
   612→    "_resolve_runtime_policy",
   613→    "execute_batch_run",
   614→    "merge_and_import_batch_run",
   615→    "prepare_batch_run",
   616→]
   617→

1→"""I/O and log helpers for review batch process execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import subprocess  # nosec
     8→import threading
     9→import time
    10→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    11→from pathlib import Path
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    14→
    15→from .types import _AttemptContext, _RunnerState
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _output_file_status_text(output_file: Path) -> str:
    21→    """Describe output file state for live log snapshots."""
    22→    if not output_file.exists():
    23→        return f"{output_file} (missing)"
    24→    try:
    25→        stat = output_file.stat()
    26→    except OSError as exc:
    27→        return f"{output_file} (exists; stat failed: {exc})"
    28→    modified_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime, tz=UTC).isoformat(
    29→        timespec="seconds"
    30→    )
    31→    return f"{output_file} (exists; bytes={stat.st_size}; modified={modified_at})"
    32→
    33→
    34→def _output_file_has_json_payload(output_file: Path) -> bool:
    35→    """Return True when the output file contains a valid JSON object."""
    36→    if not output_file.exists():
    37→        return False
    38→    try:
    39→        payload = json.loads(output_file.read_text())
    40→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
    41→        return False
    42→    return isinstance(payload, dict)
    43→
    44→
    45→def extract_payload_from_log(
    46→    batch_index: int,
    47→    raw_path: Path,
    48→    extract_fn,
    49→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
    50→    """Try to recover a batch payload from the runner log file."""
    51→    log_path = raw_path.parent.parent / "logs" / f"batch-{batch_index + 1}.log"
    52→    if not log_path.exists():
    53→        return None
    54→    try:
    55→        log_text = log_path.read_text()
    56→    except OSError:
    57→        return None
    58→
    59→    stdout_marker = "\nSTDOUT:\n"
    60→    stderr_marker = "\n\nSTDERR:\n"
    61→    stdout_start = log_text.rfind(stdout_marker)
    62→    if stdout_start == -1 and log_text.startswith("STDOUT:\n"):
    63→        stdout_start = 0
    64→        stdout_offset = len("STDOUT:\n")
    65→    elif stdout_start >= 0:
    66→        stdout_offset = len(stdout_marker)
    67→    else:
    68→        stdout_offset = 0
    69→    if stdout_start >= 0:
    70→        start_idx = stdout_start + stdout_offset
    71→        stdout_end = log_text.find(stderr_marker, start_idx)
    72→        stdout_text = (
    73→            log_text[start_idx:] if stdout_end == -1 else log_text[start_idx:stdout_end]
    74→        )
    75→        payload = extract_fn(stdout_text)
    76→        if payload is not None:
    77→            return payload
    78→        # If the batch log has a concrete STDOUT section but it contains no parseable
    79→        # payload, do not fallback to parsing the whole log. Full logs include the
    80→        # prompt template (often with JSON examples), which can hide true STDERR failures.
    81→        return None
    82→
    83→    return extract_fn(log_text)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _terminate_process(process: subprocess.Popen[str]) -> None:
    87→    """Terminate (then kill) a subprocess that may still be running."""
    88→    if process.poll() is not None:
    89→        return
    90→    try:
    91→        process.terminate()
    92→        process.wait(timeout=3)
    93→        return
    94→    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
    95→        log_best_effort_failure(
    96→            logger,
    97→            "terminate review subprocess before kill fallback",
    98→            exc,
    99→        )
   100→    try:

commit 96786dd3d455053dd8dacf699156004f98d82518
Author: jaredkirby <jaredkirby@users.noreply.github.com>
Date:   Mon Mar 9 23:33:42 2026 +0100

    fix: review packet reuse and runner hang regressions (#372)
    
    Apply preflight dimension resolution from prepared query.json so that
    run-batches and external-start modes correctly resolve target dimensions
    without requiring explicit --dimensions.  Add load_query() helper used
    by the preflight gate.
    
    Of the original 5 fixes in the PR, 3 were already resolved on main:
    - Stall detection for silent Codex batches (fixed in core refactor)
    - Run-scoped blind packet paths (addressed in runtime_paths update)
    - jscpd skip when unavailable (already implemented with _resolve_jscpd_command)
    
    The 2 fixes applied here:
    - Prepared packet reuse via load_query() helper
    - Preflight dimension resolution from query.json for run-batches mode
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit 341e0b89cafff4b6125d4db4ce0072a91bcd0c74
Author: POM <[REDACTED]>
Date:   Sun Mar 8 20:24:37 2026 +0100

    fix: resolve issues #242, #248, #263, #371 — review hangs, alias resolution, triage cleanup, batch reliability
    
    - Add early exit in `next` for empty queues and scan-completion guard in `review` (#242)
    - Walk up from scan path to find tsconfig.json for path alias resolution in orphaned detector (#248)
    - Purge workflow IDs and clear triage_recommended flag on triage completion (#263)
    - Prefer local jscpd binary, grace period for stall detection, reuse prepared query packets (#371)
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/desloppify/app/commands/review/_runner_process_io.py b/desloppify/app/commands/review/_runner_process_io.py
index 3b19678a..6abaf8cf 100644
--- a/desloppify/app/commands/review/_runner_process_io.py
+++ b/desloppify/app/commands/review/_runner_process_io.py
@@ -177,7 +177,13 @@ def _check_stall(
     last_activity: float,
     threshold: int,
 ) -> tuple[bool, tuple[int, int] | None, float | None]:
-    """Check for runner stall. Returns (stalled, new_sig, new_stable_since)."""
+    """Check for runner stall. Returns (stalled, new_sig, new_stable_since).
+
+    When no output artifact exists yet the batch is still initialising — the
+    stall detector must not declare a stall.  The caller already enforces the
+    real timeout, so batches that never produce output will still be killed;
+    they just won't be killed *early* by the stall heuristic.
+    """
     try:
         stat = output_file.stat()
         current_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = (
@@ -187,11 +193,10 @@ def _check_stall(
     except OSError:
         current_signature = None
     if current_signature is None:
+        # No output file yet — the batch hasn't started writing.  Never
+        # declare a stall in this state; just carry forward the baseline
+        # timestamp so that once the file *does* appear, we start fresh.
         baseline = prev_stable if isinstance(prev_stable, int | float) else now
-        output_age = now - baseline
-        stream_idle = now - last_activity
-        if output_age >= threshold and stream_idle >= threshold:
-            return True, None, baseline
         return False, None, baseline
     if current_signature != prev_sig:
         return False, current_signature, now

100→    try:

   105→
   106→
   107→def _drain_stream(stream, sink: list[str], state: _RunnerState) -> None:
   108→    """Read lines from *stream* into *sink*, updating activity timestamp."""
   109→    if stream is None:
   110→        return
   111→    try:
   112→        for chunk in iter(stream.readline, ""):
   113→            if not chunk:
   114→                break
   115→            with state.lock:
   116→                sink.append(chunk)
   117→                state.last_stream_activity = time.monotonic()
   118→    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   119→        with state.lock:
   120→            sink.append(f"\n[stream read error: {exc}]\n")
   121→    finally:
   122→        try:
   123→            stream.close()
   124→        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
   125→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "close review batch stream", exc)
   126→
   127→
   128→def _write_live_snapshot(state: _RunnerState, ctx: _AttemptContext) -> None:
   129→    """Write a point-in-time log snapshot while the runner is active."""
   130→    elapsed_seconds = int(max(0.0, time.monotonic() - ctx.started_monotonic))
   131→    with state.lock:
   132→        stdout_preview = "".join(state.stdout_chunks)
   133→        stderr_preview = "".join(state.stderr_chunks)
   134→        note = state.runner_note
   135→    note_block = f"\nRUNNER NOTE: {note}" if note else ""
   136→    ctx.safe_write_text_fn(
   137→        ctx.log_file,
   138→        "\n\n".join(
   139→            ctx.log_sections
   140→            + [
   141→                (
   142→                    f"{ctx.header}\n\n"
   143→                    "STATUS: running\n"
   144→                    f"STARTED AT: {ctx.started_at_iso}\n"
   145→                    f"ELAPSED: {elapsed_seconds}s\n"
   146→                    f"OUTPUT FILE: {_output_file_status_text(ctx.output_file)}"
   147→                    f"{note_block}\n\n"
   148→                    f"STDOUT (live):\n{stdout_preview}\n\n"
   149→                    f"STDERR (live):\n{stderr_preview}\n"
   150→                )
   151→            ]
   152→        ),
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→def _start_live_writer(
   157→    state: _RunnerState,
   158→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   159→    interval: float,
   160→) -> threading.Thread:
   161→    """Spawn a daemon thread that periodically writes live log snapshots."""
   162→
   163→    def _loop() -> None:
   164→        while not state.stop_event.wait(interval):
   165→            _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
   166→
   167→    thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True)
   168→    thread.start()
   169→    return thread
   170→
   171→
   172→def _check_stall(
   173→    output_file: Path,
   174→    prev_sig: tuple[int, int] | None,
   175→    prev_stable: float | None,
   176→    now: float,
   177→    last_activity: float,
   178→    threshold: int,
   179→) -> tuple[bool, tuple[int, int] | None, float | None]:
   180→    """Check for runner stall. Returns (stalled, new_sig, new_stable_since).
   181→
   182→    When no output artifact exists yet the batch is still initialising — the
   183→    stall detector must not declare a stall.  The caller already enforces the
   184→    real timeout, so batches that never produce output will still be killed;
   185→    they just won't be killed *early* by the stall heuristic.
   186→    """
   187→    try:
   188→        stat = output_file.stat()
   189→        current_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = (
   190→            int(stat.st_size),
   191→            int(stat.st_mtime),
   192→        )
   193→    except OSError:
   194→        current_signature = None
   195→    if current_signature is None:
   196→        # No output file yet — the batch hasn't started writing.  Never
   197→        # declare a stall in this state; just carry forward the baseline
   198→        # timestamp so that once the file *does* appear, we start fresh.
   199→        baseline = prev_stable if isinstance(prev_stable, int | float) else now
```

def collect_batch_results(
    *,
    request: CollectBatchResultsRequest,
    extract_payload_fn,
    normalize_result_fn,
) -> tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]:
    """Parse and normalize batch outputs, preserving prior failures."""
    selected_indexes = request.selected_indexes
    output_files = request.output_files
    allowed_dims = request.allowed_dims
    batch_results: list[BatchResult] = []
    failure_set = set(request.failures)
    for idx in selected_indexes:
        had_execution_failure = idx in failure_set
        raw_path = output_files[idx]
        payload = None
        parsed_from_log = False
        if raw_path.exists():
            try:
                payload = extract_payload_fn(raw_path.read_text())
            except OSError as exc:
                logger.warning("Failed reading batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
                payload = None
        if payload is None:
            payload = extract_payload_from_log(idx, raw_path, extract_payload_fn)
            parsed_from_log = payload is not None
        if payload is None:
            failure_set.add(idx)
            continue
        if parsed_from_log:
            try:
                safe_write_text(raw_path, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
            except OSError as exc:
                logger.warning("Failed writing normalized batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
        try:
            (
                assessments,
                issues,
                dimension_notes,
                dimension_judgment,
                quality,
                context_updates,
            ) = normalize_result_fn(payload, allowed_dims)
        except ValueError as exc:
            logger.debug("Invalid batch payload at index %s (%s): %s", idx, raw_path, exc)
            failure_set.add(idx)
            continue
        if had_execution_failure:
            failure_set.discard(idx)
            logger.info(
                "Batch #%d recovered: execution exited non-zero but raw payload parsed successfully",
```
