```
1→"""Shared triage guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.issue_id_display import short_issue_id
    10→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    11→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.context import has_objective_backlog, is_mid_cycle
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    14→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    15→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE,
    16→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX,
    17→    TriageSnapshot,
    18→    build_triage_snapshot,
    19→    triage_phase_banner,
    20→)
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = frozenset({"review", "concerns"})
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass
    27→class TriageGuardrailResult:
    28→    """Structured result from triage staleness detection."""
    29→
    30→    is_stale: bool = False
    31→    pending_behind_objective_backlog: bool = False
    32→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    33→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    34→    _snapshot: TriageSnapshot | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    35→
    36→
    37→def triage_guardrail_status(
    38→    *,
    39→    plan: dict | None = None,
    40→    state: dict | None = None,
    41→) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    42→    """Pure detection: is triage stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    43→    try:
    44→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    45→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    46→        logger.debug("Triage guardrail status skipped: plan could not be loaded.", exc_info=exc)
    47→        return TriageGuardrailResult()
    48→
    49→    resolved_state = state or {}
    50→
    51→    snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    52→    if not snapshot.is_triage_stale:
    53→        return TriageGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan, _snapshot=snapshot)
    54→
    55→    pending_behind_objective_backlog = (
    56→        not snapshot.has_triage_in_queue
    57→        and bool(resolved_state)
    58→        and is_mid_cycle(resolved_plan)
    59→        and has_objective_backlog(resolved_state, None)
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    return TriageGuardrailResult(
    63→        is_stale=True,
    64→        pending_behind_objective_backlog=pending_behind_objective_backlog,
    65→        new_ids=set(snapshot.new_since_triage_ids),
    66→        _plan=resolved_plan,
    67→        _snapshot=snapshot,
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def triage_guardrail_messages(
    72→    *,
    73→    plan: dict | None = None,
    74→    state: dict | None = None,
    75→) -> list[str]:
    76→    """Return warning strings without printing."""
    77→    resolved_state = state or {}
    78→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    79→    if not result.is_stale:
    80→        return []
    81→
    82→    messages: list[str] = []
    83→    if result.new_ids:
    84→        if result.pending_behind_objective_backlog:
    85→            messages.append(
    86→                f"{len(result.new_ids)} new review issue(s) arrived since the last triage."
    87→                " They will activate after the current objective backlog is clear."
    88→            )
    89→        else:
    90→            messages.append(
    91→                f"{len(result.new_ids)} new review issue(s) not yet triaged."
    92→                " Run the staged triage runner to incorporate them "
    93→                f"(`{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}` or `{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}`)."
    94→            )
    95→
    96→    if result._plan is not None:
    97→        banner = triage_phase_banner(result._plan, resolved_state, snapshot=result._snapshot)
    98→        if banner:
    99→            messages.append(banner)
   100→
   101→    return messages
   102→
   103→
   104→def print_triage_guardrail_info(
   105→    *,
   106→    plan: dict | None = None,
   107→    state: dict | None = None,
   108→) -> bool:
   109→    """Print yellow info banner if triage is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
   110→    messages = triage_guardrail_messages(plan=plan, state=state)
   111→    for msg in messages:
   112→        print(colorize(f"  {msg}", "yellow"))
   113→    return bool(messages)
   114→
   115→
   116→def require_triage_current_or_exit(
   117→    *,
   118→    state: dict,
   119→    plan: dict | None = None,
   120→    patterns: list[str] | None = None,
   121→    bypass: bool = False,
   122→    attest: str = "",
   123→) -> None:
   124→    """Gate: exit(1) if triage is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
   125→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
   126→    if not result.is_stale:
   127→        return
   128→
   129→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
   130→        print(colorize(
   131→            "  Triage guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
   132→            "yellow",
   133→        ))
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if result.pending_behind_objective_backlog and patterns:
   137→        matched_targets = _matched_open_targets(state, patterns)
   138→        if matched_targets and not _targets_include_review_work(matched_targets):
   139→            banner = triage_phase_banner(
   140→                result._plan or {},
   141→                state,
   142→                snapshot=result._snapshot,
   143→            )
   144→            if banner:
   145→                print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
   146→            return
   147→
   148→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   149→    if result.pending_behind_objective_backlog:
   150→        lines = [
   151→            "BLOCKED: review issues changed since the last triage, but triage is pending"
   152→            " behind the current objective backlog.",
   153→            "",
   154→            "  Finish current objective work first; triage will activate after the backlog clears.",
   155→            '  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."',
   156→        ]
   157→        raise CommandError("\n".join(lines))
   158→
   159→    lines = [
   160→        f"BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review work item(s) have not been triaged."
   161→    ]
   162→    if new_ids:
   163→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   164→            issue = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).get(fid, {})
   165→            lines.append(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
   166→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   167→            lines.append(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more")
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Codex):  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}")
   170→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Claude): {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}")
   171→    lines.append("  Manual fallback:    desloppify plan triage")
   172→    lines.append("  (Review new issues, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)")
   173→    lines.append("")
   174→    lines.append("  View new execution items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent")
   175→    lines.append("  View broader backlog:      desloppify backlog")
   176→    lines.append('  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."')
   177→    raise CommandError("\n".join(lines))
   178→
   179→
   180→def _matched_open_targets(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> list[dict]:
   181→    matched_by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   182→    for pattern in patterns:
   183→        for issue in state_mod.match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   184→            issue_id = str(issue.get("id", "")).strip()
   185→            if issue_id and issue_id not in matched_by_id:
   186→                matched_by_id[issue_id] = issue
   187→    return list(matched_by_id.values())
   188→
   189→
   190→def _targets_include_review_work(matched_targets: list[dict]) -> bool:
   191→    return any(issue.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS for issue in matched_targets)
   192→
   193→
   194→__all__ = [
   195→    "TriageGuardrailResult",
   196→    "print_triage_guardrail_info",
   197→    "require_triage_current_or_exit",
   198→    "triage_guardrail_messages",
   199→    "triage_guardrail_status",
   200→]
   201→
```

Use `gh pr diff 414` to read the full diff. Also read the PR body with `gh pr view 414 --json body -q .body`.

This is for the desloppify project — a Python CLI tool for codebase health tracking with language plugins. Key context:
- Language plugins live in `desloppify/languages/{lang}/`
- `engine/` has shared detector/scoring infrastructure
- `base/registry/` has the canonical detector registry
- Zone classification determines file intent
- Import direction: base/ imports nothing from engine/app. engine/ imports from base/ only.

Review for:
1. Architecture — is the shared framework detection layer well-structured? Does it follow import direction rules?
2. Correctness — does Next.js detection work properly? Is `next lint` integration sound?
3. Code quality — clean, follows existing patterns? Any dead code or over-engineering?
4. Test coverage — adequate?
5. Size/scope — is this PR too large? Should it be split?

DO NOT make any changes. Just provide a thorough review summary with specific file:line references for any issues found.

```
    65→
    66→def triage_phase_banner(
    67→    plan: PlanModel,
    68→    state: dict | None = None,
    69→    *,
    70→    snapshot: TriageSnapshot | None = None,
    71→) -> str:
    72→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    73→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    74→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    75→    run_hint = (
    76→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    77→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    78→    )
    79→    resolved_state = state or {}
    80→    resolved_snapshot = snapshot or build_triage_snapshot(plan, resolved_state)
    81→
    82→    if not resolved_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
    83→        if (
    84→            resolved_state
    85→            and resolved_snapshot.is_triage_stale
    86→            and is_mid_cycle(plan)
    87→            and has_objective_backlog(resolved_state, None)
    88→        ):
    89→            return (
    90→                "TRIAGE PENDING — review issues changed since last triage and will "
    91→                "activate after objective work is complete."
    92→            )
    93→        undispositioned = len(resolved_snapshot.undispositioned_ids)
    94→        if undispositioned:
    95→            return (
    96→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    97→                f"{undispositioned} review work item(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    98→                f"{run_hint}"
    99→            )
   100→        if resolved_snapshot.is_triage_stale or meta.get("triage_recommended"):
   101→            return (
   102→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review work items changed since last triage. "
   103→                f"{run_hint}"
   104→            )
   105→        return ""
   106→
   107→    if resolved_state and has_objective_backlog(resolved_state, None):
   108→        undispositioned = len(resolved_snapshot.undispositioned_ids)
   109→        if undispositioned:
   110→            return (
   111→                "TRIAGE PENDING — "
   112→                f"{undispositioned} review work item(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions after current work. "
   113→                f"{run_hint}"
   114→            )
   115→        return (
   116→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
   117→            "is complete."
   118→        )
   119→    progress = resolved_snapshot.progress
   120→    if progress.completed_count:
   121→        total_stages = len(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   122→        return (
   123→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({progress.completed_count}/{total_stages} stages recorded) — "
   124→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
   125→        )
   126→    return (
   127→        "TRIAGE MODE — review work items need analysis before fixing. "
   128→        f"{run_hint}"
   129→    )
   130→
```

```
    17→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.resolution import coerce_assessment_score
    19→from desloppify.state_io import load_state
    20→
    21→from .apply import _resolve_all_patterns, _write_resolve_query_entry
    22→from .living_plan import update_living_plan_after_resolve
    23→from .messages import print_fixed_next_user_message, print_no_match_warning
    24→from .plan_load import load_resolve_plan_access
    25→from .queue_guard import _check_queue_order_guard
    26→from .render import (
    27→    _print_next_command,
    28→    _print_resolve_summary,
    29→    _print_subjective_reset_hint,
    30→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning,
    31→    render_commit_guidance,
    32→)
    33→from .selection import (
    34→    ResolveQueryContext,
    35→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation,
    36→    _previous_score_snapshot,
    37→    _validate_resolve_inputs,
    38→)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _validate_fixed_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    42→    if args.status != "fixed":
    43→        return True
    44→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    45→    if validate_note_length(note):
    46→        return True
    47→    show_note_length_requirement(note)
    48→    return False
    49→
    50→
    51→def _load_state_with_guards(
    52→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    53→    *,
    54→    attestation: str | None,
    55→) -> tuple[str, dict, object] | None:
    56→    """Validate inputs, apply guardrails, and load state for resolve."""
    57→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    58→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
    59→        return None
    60→
    61→    state_file = state_path(args)
    62→    state = load_state(state_file)
    63→    plan_access = load_resolve_plan_access()
    64→
    65→    if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
    66→        if _check_queue_order_guard(
    67→            state,
    68→            args.patterns,
    69→            args.status,
    70→            plan_access=plan_access,
    71→        ):
    72→            return None
    73→
    74→    if args.status == "fixed":
    75→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
    76→            state=state,
    77→            plan=plan_access.plan if isinstance(plan_access.plan, dict) and not plan_access.degraded else None,
    78→            patterns=args.patterns,
    79→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
    80→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
    81→        )
    82→
    83→    return state_file, state, plan_access
    84→
    85→
    86→def _resolve_ids_with_snapshots(
    87→    state: dict,
    88→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    89→    *,
    90→    attestation: str | None,
    91→    plan_access,
    92→) -> tuple[object, dict[str, float], list[str]]:
    93→    """Apply resolve patterns and return pre/post context for rendering."""
    94→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    95→        state,
    96→        args,
    97→        attestation=attestation,
    98→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    99→    )
   100→
   101→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   102→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   103→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   104→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   105→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   106→    }
   107→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(
   108→        state,
   109→        args,
   110→        attestation=attestation,
   111→        plan_access=plan_access,
   112→    )
   113→    return prev, prev_subjective_scores, all_resolved
   114→
   115→
   116→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   117→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
   118→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   119→    loaded = _load_state_with_guards(args, attestation=attestation)
   120→    if loaded is None:
   121→        return
   122→    state_file, state, plan_access = loaded
   123→    prev, prev_subjective_scores, all_resolved = _resolve_ids_with_snapshots(
   124→        state,
   125→        args,
   126→        attestation=attestation,
   127→        plan_access=plan_access,
   128→    )
   129→    if not all_resolved:
   130→        print_no_match_warning(args)
   131→        return
   132→
   133→    save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   134→
   135→    plan, cluster_ctx = update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   136→        args=args,
   137→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   138→        attestation=attestation,
   139→        state=state,
   140→        state_file=state_file,
   141→    )
   142→    mid_cluster = (
   143→        cluster_ctx.cluster_name is not None and not cluster_ctx.cluster_completed
   144→    )
   145→
   146→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   147→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   148→        state,
   149→        status=args.status,
   150→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   151→    )
   152→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   153→    if not mid_cluster:
   154→        render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   155→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   156→        args=args,
   157→        state=state,
   158→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   159→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   160→    )
   161→
   162→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   163→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   164→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   165→        state,
   166→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   167→    )
   168→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   169→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   170→
   171→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   172→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   173→        ResolveQueryContext(
   174→            patterns=args.patterns,
   175→            status=args.status,
   176→            resolved=all_resolved,
   177→            next_command=next_command,
   178→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   179→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   180→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   181→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   182→            attestation=attestation,
   183→            narrative=narrative,
   184→            state=state,
   185→        )
   186→    )
   187→    print_fixed_next_user_message(
   188→        args=args,
   189→        plan=plan,
   190→        next_command=next_command,
   191→        mid_cluster=mid_cluster,
   192→        cluster_ctx=cluster_ctx,
   193→    )
   194→
   195→
   196→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard", "cmd_resolve"]
   197→
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether a planned objective backlog exists.
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available (plan-aware:
    32→    only counts planned objectives post-triage).  Falls back to scanning
    33→    *state_or_issues* directly (counts all open non-subjective issues).
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"work_items": ...}`) or a raw
    39→    # work-item mapping, with legacy ``issues`` as a fallback alias.
    40→    issues = state_or_issues
    41→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    42→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("work_items")
    43→        if not isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    44→            maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    45→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    46→            issues = maybe_issues
    47→    return any(
    48→        f.get("status") == "open"
    49→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    50→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    51→        for f in issues.values()
    52→    )
    53→
    54→
    55→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    56→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    57→
    58→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    59→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    60→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    61→    """
    62→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    63→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    64→
    65→
    66→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    67→
```

```
# PR: Next.js framework smell depth + shared framework detection + JS parity

## Summary
Before this branch, Desloppify had no dedicated Next.js framework detector layer. Next.js repos were only evaluated by generic TypeScript/Javascript detectors (smells, coupling, security, etc.).

This PR adds a shared TypeScript framework detection layer and deep Next.js framework smell scanning, then reuses that stack for JavaScript so Next.js issues are detected consistently across TS/JS projects.

It also integrates `next lint` as a framework-level quality gate: when Next.js is primary and lint cannot run, we emit a `next_lint::unavailable` issue instead of silently skipping.

## Root Cause
1. There were no explicit Next.js-specific detector checks before this work; framework misuse was effectively invisible unless it happened to be caught by generic TS/JS detectors.
2. Framework detection for external scan paths could bind to the workspace root and miss the scanned repo’s `package.json`.
3. `use_server_not_first` produced false positives for valid inline server actions.

## What Changed
### Shared framework layer
- Added shared TS framework detection package:
  - `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/*`
- Added Next.js framework info + shared phase orchestration:
  - `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/nextjs/info.py`
  - `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/nextjs/phase.py`
  - `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/nextjs/scanners.py`

### Next.js lint integration (enforced behavior)
- Added framework node helper:
  - `desloppify/languages/_framework/node/next_lint.py`
- Behavior:
  - Runs `npx --no-install next lint --format json`.
  - If lint is unavailable/unparseable, emits `next_lint::unavailable` (failing issue).

### JavaScript parity
- Added JS Next.js phase:
  - `desloppify/languages/javascript/phases_nextjs.py`
- Inserted phase into JS plugin pipeline:
  - `desloppify/languages/javascript/__init__.py`

### Detector and scoring wiring
- Registered `nextjs` and `next_lint` in detector catalog and display ordering.
- Added file-based scoring policy entries for both detectors.

### Scanner depth (Next.js)
Includes high-value checks for:
- Server-only imports in client modules
- Server-only exports from client modules
- Pages APIs under `app/`
- `next/navigation` usage in `pages/`
- Client env leakage (`process.env` non-`NEXT_PUBLIC_*`)
- Route handler/middleware misuse
- `next/head` misuse in App Router
- `next/document` misuse
- Missing `use client` where needed
- Client layout and async-client smells
- Directive placement checks

### False-positive fixes
- Fixed module-level `use server` placement logic so valid nested inline server actions are not flagged.

### External path detection fix
- Framework detection now properly resolves nearest `package.json` for external scan targets.

## Impacted Shared Surfaces
- Framework detection core:
  - `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/detect.py`
- TS smells phase integration point:
  - `desloppify/languages/typescript/phases_smells.py`
- Generic discovery helper additions:
  - `desloppify/base/discovery/source.py`
- Detector registry/scoring:
  - `desloppify/base/registry/catalog_entries.py`
  - `desloppify/base/registry/catalog_models.py`
  - `desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py`

## Why This Is Systemic
- The fix centralizes framework detection and Next.js smell mapping into shared abstractions, then reuses them in both TS and JS language plugins.
- It avoids per-framework/per-language hardcoding at the callsite level.
- It enforces lint participation through a detector issue path rather than optional logging.

## Regression Protection
### Added/updated tests
- `desloppify/languages/typescript/tests/test_ts_nextjs_framework.py`
  - framework primary detection
  - external scan path detection
  - broad Next.js smell coverage
  - nested inline server-action false-positive guard
- `desloppify/languages/javascript/tests/test_js_nextjs_framework.py`
  - JS plugin phase insertion
  - JS Next.js smell emission
- Updated supporting tests for policy/runtime expectations:
  - `desloppify/tests/review/work_queue_cases.py`
  - `desloppify/languages/typescript/tests/test_ts_unused.py`

### Test result
- `6051 passed, 145 skipped`

## OSS Benchmark (baseline vs this branch)
All scans run with: `--lang typescript --profile objective --skip-slow --no-badge`

Important context: baseline has `nextjs=0` in these comparisons because baseline did not include a Next.js-specific detector path.

### Control repos (no expected Next.js smells)
1. `vercel/next-app-router-playground`
   - baseline: `nextjs=0`, new: `nextjs=0`
2. `vercel/nextjs-subscription-payments`
   - baseline: `nextjs=0`, new: `nextjs=0`

### Issue-bearing repos
1. `shadcn-ui/taxonomy`
   - baseline: `nextjs=0`
   - new: `nextjs=1`
   - finding: mixed routers (`app/` + `pages/`) project smell
2. `descope-sample-apps/linkedin-sample-app`
   - baseline: `nextjs=0`
   - new: `nextjs=2`
   - findings:
     - App Router page imports `next/head`
     - App Router root layout is client component (`"use client"`)

## Notes
- This branch is ready for PR creation after your review of this draft.
```

```
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.detectors.coverage.mapping import (
     6→    analyze_test_quality,

     9→    transitive_coverage,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap
    12→
    13→from .discovery import (
    14→    _discover_scorable_and_tests,
    15→    _no_tests_issues,
    16→    _normalize_graph_paths,
    17→)
    18→from .heuristics import _has_inline_tests
    19→from .issues import (
    20→    _generate_issues,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→def detect_test_coverage(
    25→    graph: dict,
    26→    zone_map: FileZoneMap,
    27→    lang_name: str,
    28→    extra_test_files: set[str] | None = None,
    29→    complexity_map: dict[str, float] | None = None,
    30→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    31→    graph = _normalize_graph_paths(graph)
    32→
    33→    production_files, test_files, scorable, potential = _discover_scorable_and_tests(
    34→        graph=graph,
    35→        zone_map=zone_map,
    36→        lang_name=lang_name,
    37→        extra_test_files=extra_test_files,
    38→    )
    39→    if not scorable:
    40→        return [], 0
    41→
    42→    inline_tested = {
    43→        filepath
    44→        for filepath in scorable
    45→        if filepath in production_files and _has_inline_tests(filepath, lang_name)
    46→    }
    47→
    48→    if not test_files and not inline_tested:
    49→        entries = _no_tests_issues(scorable, graph, lang_name, complexity_map)
    50→        return entries, potential
    51→
    52→    directly_tested = set(inline_tested)
    53→    if test_files:
    54→        directly_tested |= import_based_mapping(
    55→            graph,
    56→            test_files,
    57→            production_files,
    58→            lang_name,
    59→        )
    60→        directly_tested |= naming_based_mapping(test_files, production_files, lang_name)
    61→
    62→    transitively_tested = transitive_coverage(directly_tested, graph, production_files)
    63→    test_quality = analyze_test_quality(test_files, lang_name)
    64→
    65→    entries = _generate_issues(
    66→        scorable,
    67→        directly_tested,
    68→        transitively_tested,
    69→        test_quality,
    70→        graph,
    71→        lang_name,
    72→        complexity_map=complexity_map,
    73→    )
    74→    return entries, potential
    75→
```

```
1→"""Import-resolution helpers shared by coverage mapping."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.detectors.test_coverage.io import read_coverage_file
     8→from desloppify.engine.hook_registry import get_lang_hook
     9→
    10→
    11→def _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name: str | None):
    12→    """Load language-specific test coverage helpers from ``lang/<name>/test_coverage.py``."""
    13→    return get_lang_hook(lang_name, "test_coverage") or object()
    14→
    15→
    16→def _infer_lang_name(test_files: set[str], production_files: set[str]) -> str | None:
    17→    """Infer language from known file extensions when explicit lang is unavailable."""
    18→    paths = list(test_files) + list(production_files)
    19→    ext_to_lang = {
    20→        ".py": "python",
    21→        ".pyi": "python",
    22→        ".ts": "typescript",
    23→        ".tsx": "typescript",
    24→        ".js": "typescript",
    25→        ".jsx": "typescript",
    26→        ".cs": "csharp",
    27→        ".php": "php",
    28→        ".go": "go",
    29→        ".rs": "rust",
    30→    }
    31→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    32→    for file_path in paths:
    33→        suffix = Path(file_path).suffix.lower()
    34→        lang_name = ext_to_lang.get(suffix)
    35→        if not lang_name:
    36→            continue
    37→        counts[lang_name] = counts.get(lang_name, 0) + 1
    38→    if counts:
    39→        return max(counts.items(), key=lambda item: item[1])[0]
    40→    return None
    41→
    42→
    43→def _resolve_import(
    44→    spec: str,
    45→    test_path: str,
    46→    production_files: set[str],
    47→    lang_name: str | None,
    48→) -> str | None:
    49→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
    50→    resolver = getattr(mod, "resolve_import_spec", None)
    51→    if callable(resolver):
    52→        return resolver(spec, test_path, production_files)
    53→    return None
    54→
    55→
    56→def _resolve_barrel_reexports(
    57→    filepath: str,
    58→    production_files: set[str],
    59→    lang_name: str | None = None,
    60→) -> set[str]:
    61→    """Resolve one-hop re-exports using language-specific helpers."""
    62→    if lang_name is None:
    63→        lang_name = _infer_lang_name({filepath}, production_files)
    64→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
    65→    resolver = getattr(mod, "resolve_barrel_reexports", None)
    66→    if callable(resolver):
    67→        return resolver(filepath, production_files)
    68→    return set()
    69→
    70→
    71→def _parse_test_imports(
    72→    test_path: str,
    73→    production_files: set[str],
    74→    prod_by_module: dict[str, str],
    75→    lang_name: str | None = None,
    76→) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Parse import statements from a test file and resolve production files."""
    78→    tested = set()
    79→    read_result = read_coverage_file(test_path, context="coverage_import_mapping_parse")
    80→    if not read_result.ok:
    81→        return tested
    82→    content = read_result.content
    83→
    84→    if lang_name is None:
    85→        lang_name = _infer_lang_name({test_path}, production_files)
    86→
    87→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
    88→    parse_specs = getattr(mod, "parse_test_import_specs", None)
    89→    if not callable(parse_specs):
    90→        return tested
    91→
    92→    for spec in parse_specs(content):
    93→        if not spec:
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        resolved = _resolve_import(spec, test_path, production_files, lang_name)
    97→        if resolved:
    98→            tested.add(resolved)
    99→            continue
   100→
   101→        # Fallback: module-name lookup with progressively shorter prefixes.
   102→        cleaned = spec.lstrip("./").replace("/", ".")
   103→        parts = cleaned.split(".")
   104→        for i in range(len(parts), 0, -1):
   105→            candidate = ".".join(parts[:i])
   106→            if candidate in prod_by_module:
   107→                tested.add(prod_by_module[candidate])
   108→                break
   109→
   110→    return tested
   111→
   112→
   113→__all__ = [
   114→    "_infer_lang_name",
   115→    "_load_lang_test_coverage_module",
   116→    "_parse_test_imports",
   117→    "_resolve_barrel_reexports",
   118→]
   119→
```

```
140→    """Extract the usable name from an import path.
   141→
   142→    Examples:
   143→        "fmt" -> "fmt"
   144→        "./utils" -> "utils"
   145→        "crate::module::Foo" -> "Foo"
   146→        "com.example.MyClass" -> "MyClass"
   147→        "MyApp::Model::User" -> "User"
   148→        "Data.List" -> "List"
   149→    """
   150→    # Strip common path separators and take the last segment.
   151→    for sep in ("::", ".", "/", "\\"):
   152→        if sep in import_path:
   153→            parts = import_path.split(sep)
   154→            # Filter out empty segments and take the last.
   155→            parts = [p for p in parts if p]
   156→            if parts:
   157→                name = parts[-1]
   158→                # Strip file extensions.
   159→                for ext in (".go", ".rs", ".rb", ".py", ".js", ".jsx", ".ts",
   160→                            ".tsx", ".java", ".kt", ".cs", ".fs", ".ml",
   161→                            ".ex", ".erl", ".hs", ".lua", ".zig", ".pm",
   162→                            ".sh", ".pl", ".scala", ".swift", ".php",
   163→                            ".dart", ".mjs", ".cjs"):
   164→                    if name.endswith(ext):
   165→                        name = name[:-len(ext)]
   166→                        break
   167→                return name
   168→
   169→    # No separator — the path itself is the name.
   170→    return import_path
   171→
   172→
   173→__all__ = ["detect_unused_imports"]
   174→
```

```
1→"""C/C++-specific test coverage heuristics and mappings."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import re
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.text_utils import strip_c_style_comments
    10→
    11→ASSERT_PATTERNS = [re.compile(r"\bASSERT_[A-Z_]+\b"), re.compile(r"\bEXPECT_[A-Z_]+\b")]
    12→MOCK_PATTERNS = [re.compile(r"\bMOCK_METHOD\b"), re.compile(r"\bFakeIt\b")]
    13→SNAPSHOT_PATTERNS: list[re.Pattern[str]] = []
    14→TEST_FUNCTION_RE = re.compile(r"\bTEST(?:_F|_P)?\s*\(")
    15→BARREL_BASENAMES: set[str] = set()
    16→_INCLUDE_RE = re.compile(r'(?m)^\s*#include\s*[<"]([^>"]+)[>"]')
    17→_SOURCE_EXTENSIONS = (".c", ".cc", ".cpp", ".cxx")
    18→_TESTABLE_LOGIC_RE = re.compile(
    19→    r"\b(?:class|struct|enum|namespace)\b|^\s*(?:inline\s+|static\s+)?[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:[\s*&:<>]+[A-Za-z_]\w*)*\s+\w+\s*\(",
    20→    re.MULTILINE,
    21→ )
    22→
    23→
    24→def has_testable_logic(filepath: str, content: str) -> bool:
    25→    """Return True when a file looks like it contains runtime logic."""
    26→    basename = os.path.basename(filepath)
    27→    if filepath.endswith(("_test.c", "_test.cc", "_test.cpp", "_test.cxx")):
    28→        return False
    29→    if basename.startswith("test_") and basename.endswith(_SOURCE_EXTENSIONS):
    30→        return False
    31→    return bool(_TESTABLE_LOGIC_RE.search(content))
    32→
    33→
    34→def _match_candidate(candidate: Path, production_files: set[str]) -> str | None:
    35→    resolved = str(candidate.resolve())
    36→    normalized = {str(Path(path).resolve()): path for path in production_files}
    37→    if resolved in normalized:
    38→        return normalized[resolved]
    39→    return None
    40→
    41→
    42→def resolve_import_spec(
    43→    spec: str,
    44→    test_path: str,
    45→    production_files: set[str],
    46→) -> str | None:
    47→    """Resolve include-like specs used in C/C++ tests."""
    48→    cleaned = (spec or "").strip().strip("\"'")
    49→    if not cleaned:
    50→        return None
    51→
    52→    test_file = Path(test_path).resolve()
    53→    candidates: list[Path] = []
    54→    if cleaned.startswith("./") or cleaned.startswith("../"):
    55→        candidates.append((test_file.parent / cleaned).resolve())
    56→    else:
    57→        candidates.append((test_file.parent / cleaned).resolve())
    58→        leaf = Path(cleaned).name
    59→        for production in production_files:
    60→            if Path(production).name == leaf:
    61→                return production
    62→
    63→    for candidate in candidates:
    64→        matched = _match_candidate(candidate, production_files)
    65→        if matched:
    66→            return matched
    67→    return None
    68→
    69→
    70→def resolve_barrel_reexports(filepath: str, production_files: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    71→    """C/C++ has no barrel-file re-export expansion."""
    72→    del filepath, production_files
    73→    return set()
    74→
    75→
    76→def parse_test_import_specs(content: str) -> list[str]:
    77→    """Return include-like specs from test content."""
    78→    return [match.group(1).strip() for match in _INCLUDE_RE.finditer(content)]
    79→
    80→
    81→def map_test_to_source(test_path: str, production_set: set[str]) -> str | None:
    82→    """Map a C/C++ test file to its likely source counterpart."""
    83→    basename = os.path.basename(test_path)
    84→    src_name = strip_test_markers(basename)
    85→    if not src_name:
    86→        return None
    87→
    88→    test_file = Path(test_path).resolve()
    89→    candidates = [
    90→        test_file.with_name(src_name),
    91→        test_file.parent.parent / src_name,
    92→        test_file.parent.parent / "src" / src_name,
    93→        test_file.parent.parent / "source" / src_name,
    94→        test_file.parent.parent / "lib" / src_name,
    95→    ]
    96→    for candidate in candidates:
    97→        matched = _match_candidate(candidate, production_set)
    98→        if matched:
    99→            return matched
   100→
   101→    for production in production_set:
   102→        if Path(production).name == src_name and not re.search(r"(?:^|[\\/])tests?(?:[\\/]|$)", production):
   103→            return production
   104→    return None
   105→
   106→
   107→def strip_test_markers(basename: str) -> str | None:
   108→    """Strip common C/C++ test-name markers to derive source basename."""
   109→    stem, ext = os.path.splitext(basename)
   110→    if ext.lower() not in _SOURCE_EXTENSIONS:
   111→        return None
   112→    if stem.endswith("_test"):
   113→        return f"{stem[:-5]}{ext}"
   114→    if stem.startswith("test_"):
   115→        return f"{stem[5:]}{ext}"
   116→    if stem.endswith("Tests"):
   117→        return f"{stem[:-5]}{ext}"
   118→    if stem.endswith("Test"):
   119→        return f"{stem[:-4]}{ext}"
   120→    return None
   121→
   122→
   123→def strip_comments(content: str) -> str:
   124→    """Strip C-style comments while preserving string literals."""
   125→    return strip_c_style_comments(content)
   126→
```

```
   122→   122→diff --git a/desloppify/languages/_framework/node/next_lint.py b/desloppify/languages/_framework/node/next_lint.py
   123→   123→new file mode 100644
   124→   124→index 00000000..cd2174ae

   126→   126→+++ b/desloppify/languages/_framework/node/next_lint.py
   127→   127→@@ -0,0 +1,120 @@
   128→   128→+"""Next.js `next lint` integration.
   129→   129→+
   130→   130→+This is treated as a framework-level smell source for Next.js projects. It
   131→   131→+parses ESLint JSON output and maps it into per-file issues to keep state size
   132→   132→+bounded.
   133→   133→+"""
   134→   134→+
   135→   135→+from __future__ import annotations
   136→   136→+
   137→   137→+import json
   138→   138→+import logging
   139→   139→+import shutil
   140→   140→+import subprocess  # nosec B404
   141→   141→+from pathlib import Path
   142→   142→+
   143→   143→+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   144→   144→+_proc_runtime = subprocess
   145→   145→+
   146→   146→+
   147→   147→+def _extract_json_array(text: str) -> str | None:
   148→   148→+    """Best-effort: return the first JSON array substring in *text*."""
   149→   149→+    start = text.find("[")
   150→   150→+    if start == -1:
   151→   151→+        return None
   152→   152→+    end = text.rfind("]")
   153→   153→+    if end == -1 or end <= start:
   154→   154→+        return None
   155→   155→+    return text[start : end + 1]
   156→   156→+
   157→   157→+
   158→   158→+def _relativize(filepath: str, project_root: Path) -> str:
   159→   159→+    try:
   160→   160→+        p = Path(filepath)
   161→   161→+        if p.is_absolute():
   162→   162→+            return str(p.resolve().relative_to(project_root.resolve()))
   163→   163→+    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
   164→   164→+        pass
   165→   165→+    return filepath
   166→   166→+
   167→   167→+
   168→   168→+def run_next_lint(project_root: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], int, str | None]:
   169→   169→+    """Run `next lint --format json` and return (entries, potential, error).
   170→   170→+
   171→   171→+    - entries are *per-file* aggregates: {file, line, message, count, messages}
   172→   172→+    - potential is the number of files lint reported on (len(json array))
   173→   173→+    - error is a short string when lint could not be run/parsed
   174→   174→+    """
   175→   175→+    npx_path = shutil.which("npx")
   176→   176→+    if not npx_path:
   177→   177→+        return [], 0, "npx executable not found in PATH"
   178→   178→+
   179→   179→+    cmd = [npx_path, "--no-install", "next", "lint", "--format", "json"]
   180→   180→+    try:
   181→   181→+        result = _proc_runtime.run(  # nosec B603
   182→   182→+            cmd,
   183→   183→+            capture_output=True,
   184→   184→+            text=True,
   185→   185→+            cwd=project_root,
   186→   186→+            timeout=180,
   187→   187→+        )
   188→   188→+    except (_proc_runtime.SubprocessError, OSError) as exc:
   189→   189→+        return [], 0, f"failed to run next lint: {exc}"
   190→   190→+
   191→   191→+    raw = (result.stdout or "").strip()
   192→   192→+    if not raw:
   193→   193→+        raw = (result.stderr or "").strip()
   194→   194→+    json_text = _extract_json_array(raw)
   195→   195→+    if not json_text:
   196→   196→+        return [], 0, "next lint did not return JSON output (try installing dependencies)"
   197→   197→+
   198→   198→+    try:
   199→   199→+        data = json.loads(json_text)
   200→   200→+    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
   201→   201→+        logger.debug("next lint JSON parse failed: %s", exc)
   202→   202→+        return [], 0, "could not parse next lint JSON output"
   203→   203→+
   204→   204→+    if not isinstance(data, list):
   205→   205→+        return [], 0, "next lint JSON output was not a list"
   206→   206→+
   207→   207→+    potential = len(data)
   208→   208→+    entries: list[dict] = []
   209→   209→+    for fobj in data:
   210→   210→+        if not isinstance(fobj, dict):
   211→   211→+            continue
   212→   212→+        file_path = fobj.get("filePath") or ""
   213→   213→+        messages = fobj.get("messages") or []
   214→   214→+        if not file_path or not isinstance(messages, list) or not messages:
   215→   215→+            continue
   216→   216→+
   217→   217→+        rel = _relativize(str(file_path), project_root)
   218→   218→+        first = next((m for m in messages if isinstance(m, dict)), None)
   219→   219→+        if first is None:
   220→   220→+            continue
   221→   221→+        line = first.get("line") if isinstance(first.get("line"), int) else 1
   222→   222→+        msg = first.get("message") if isinstance(first.get("message"), str) else "Lint issue"
   223→   223→+        entries.append(
   224→   224→+            {
   225→   225→+                "file": rel,
   226→   226→+                "line": line if line and line > 0 else 1,
   227→   227→+                "message": msg,
   228→   228→+                "count": len(messages),
   229→   229→+                "messages": [
   230→   230→+                    {
   231→   231→+                        "line": m.get("line", 0),
   232→   232→+                        "column": m.get("column", 0),
   233→   233→+                        "ruleId": m.get("ruleId", ""),
   234→   234→+                        "message": m.get("message", ""),
   235→   235→+                        "severity": m.get("severity", 0),
   236→   236→+                    }
   237→   237→+                    for m in messages
   238→   238→+                    if isinstance(m, dict)
   239→   239→+                ][:50],
   240→   240→+            }
   241→   241→+        )
   242→   242→+
   243→   243→+    return entries, potential, None
   244→   244→+
   245→   245→+
   246→   246→+__all__ = ["run_next_lint"]
   247→   247→+

   284→   284→diff --git a/desloppify/languages/javascript/phases_nextjs.py b/desloppify/languages/javascript/phases_nextjs.py
   285→   285→new file mode 100644
   286→   286→index 00000000..95d26834

   288→   288→+++ b/desloppify/languages/javascript/phases_nextjs.py
   289→   289→@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
   290→   290→+"""Next.js framework smells for JavaScript/JSX projects.
   291→   291→+
   292→   292→+This phase intentionally reuses the shared (data-driven) framework detector and
   293→   293→+Next.js smell scanners used by the TypeScript plugin so JS-only repos get the
   294→   294→+same depth.
   295→   295→+"""
   296→   296→+
   297→   297→+from __future__ import annotations
   298→   298→+
   299→   299→+from pathlib import Path
   300→   300→+
   301→   301→+from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
   302→   302→+from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangRuntimeContract
   303→   303→+from desloppify.languages.typescript.frameworks import detect_primary_ts_framework
   304→   304→+from desloppify.languages.typescript.frameworks.nextjs import nextjs_info_from_detection
   305→   305→+from desloppify.languages.typescript.frameworks.nextjs.phase import (
   306→   306→+    detect_nextjs_framework_smells,
   307→   307→+)
   308→   308→+from desloppify.state_io import Issue
   309→   309→+
   310→   310→+
   311→   311→+def phase_nextjs(
   312→   312→+    path: Path,
   313→   313→+    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   314→   314→+) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   315→   315→+    framework = detect_primary_ts_framework(path, lang)
   316→   316→+    nextjs_info = nextjs_info_from_detection(framework)
   317→   317→+    if not nextjs_info.is_primary:
   318→   318→+        return [], {}
   319→   319→+
   320→   320→+    scan_root = nextjs_info.package_root
   321→   321→+    return detect_nextjs_framework_smells(scan_root, nextjs_info, log)
   322→   322→+
   323→   323→+
   324→   324→+__all__ = ["phase_nextjs"]
   325→   325→+

   370→   370→+def test_phase_nextjs_emits_smells_when_nextjs_is_primary(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch):

   385→   385→+

   436→   436→+"""Framework support for TypeScript projects (Next.js, Vite, etc)."""
   437→   437→+
   438→   438→+from __future__ import annotations

   440→   440→+from .detect import detect_primary_ts_framework

   457→   457→+This is intentionally data-driven so framework selection isn't hard-coded into
   458→   458→+individual feature phases.
   459→   459→+"""

   461→   461→+from __future__ import annotations
   462→   462→+
   463→   463→+from dataclasses import dataclass
   464→   464→+
   465→   465→+from .types import FrameworkId

   467→   467→+
   468→   468→+@dataclass(frozen=True)

   471→   471→+    label: str
   472→   472→+    dependencies: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   473→   473→+    dev_dependencies: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   474→   474→+    config_files: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   475→   475→+    marker_files: tuple[str, ...] = ()

   480→   480→+    weight_marker: int = 2
   481→   481→+    weight_script: int = 1
   482→   482→+
   483→   483→+    primary_score_threshold: int = 5
   484→   484→+
   485→   485→+

   492→   492→+        marker_files=("next-env.d.ts",),
   493→   493→+        marker_dirs=("app", "src/app", "pages", "src/pages"),

   517→   517→+"""Generic framework detection for TypeScript projects."""
   518→   518→+
   519→   519→+from __future__ import annotations
   520→   520→+
   521→   521→+import json
   522→   522→+import re
   523→   523→+from pathlib import Path
   524→   524→+from typing import Any
   525→   525→+
   526→   526→+from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   527→   527→+from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangRuntimeContract
   528→   528→+

   530→   530→+from .types import FrameworkCandidate, FrameworkId, PrimaryFrameworkDetection
   531→   531→+
   532→   532→+[REDACTED]
   533→   533→+
   534→   534→+
   535→   535→+def _find_nearest_package_json(scan_path: Path, project_root: Path) -> Path | None:
   536→   536→+    resolved = scan_path if scan_path.is_absolute() else (project_root / scan_path)
   537→   537→+    resolved = resolved.resolve()
   538→   538→+    if resolved.is_file():
   539→   539→+        resolved = resolved.parent
   540→   540→+
   541→   541→+    # If scan_path is inside runtime project root, cap traversal there.
   542→   542→+    # Otherwise (e.g. --path /tmp/other-repo), traverse from scan_path upward.
   543→   543→+    limit_to_project_root = False
   544→   544→+    try:
   545→   545→+        resolved.relative_to(project_root)
   546→   546→+        limit_to_project_root = True
   547→   547→+    except ValueError:
   548→   548→+        limit_to_project_root = False
   549→   549→+
   550→   550→+    cur = resolved
   551→   551→+    while True:
   552→   552→+        candidate = cur / "package.json"
   553→   553→+        if candidate.is_file():
   554→   554→+            return candidate
   555→   555→+        if (limit_to_project_root and cur == project_root) or cur.parent == cur:
   556→   556→+            break
   557→   557→+        cur = cur.parent
   558→   558→+
   559→   559→+    # Fallback only when no package.json exists in the scanned tree.
   560→   560→+    candidate = project_root / "package.json"
   561→   561→+    return candidate if candidate.is_file() else None
   562→   562→+
   563→   563→+
   564→   564→+def _read_package_json(package_json: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
   565→   565→+    try:
   566→   566→+        payload = json.loads(package_json.read_text())
   567→   567→+    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   568→   568→+        return {}
   569→   569→+    return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
   570→   570→+
   571→   571→+
   572→   572→+def _dep_set(payload: dict[str, Any], key: str) -> set[str]:
   573→   573→+    deps = payload.get(key)
   574→   574→+    if not isinstance(deps, dict):
   575→   575→+        return set()
   576→   576→+    return {str(k) for k in deps.keys()}
   577→   577→+
   578→   578→+
   579→   579→+def _script_values(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
   580→   580→+    scripts = payload.get("scripts")
   581→   581→+    if not isinstance(scripts, dict):
   582→   582→+        return []
   583→   583→+    return [v for v in scripts.values() if isinstance(v, str)]
   584→   584→+
   585→   585→+
   586→   586→+def _existing_relpaths(
   587→   587→+    package_root: Path,
   588→   588→+    project_root: Path,
   589→   589→+    candidates: tuple[str, ...],
   590→   590→+    *,
   591→   591→+    kind: str,
   592→   592→+) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   593→   593→+    hits: list[str] = []
   594→   594→+    for relpath in candidates:
   595→   595→+        path = (package_root / relpath).resolve()
   596→   596→+        ok = path.is_dir() if kind == "dir" else path.is_file()
   597→   597→+        if not ok:
   598→   598→+            continue
   599→   599→+        try:
   600→   600→+            hits.append(path.relative_to(project_root).as_posix())
```

```
   664→   664→+def detect_primary_ts_framework(

   671→   671→+    cache_key = f"{_CACHE_KEY}:{Path(scan_path).resolve().as_posix()}"
   672→   672→+    if lang is not None:
   673→   673→+        cache = getattr(lang, "review_cache", None)

   677→   677→+                return cached

   681→   681→+    package_root = (package_json.parent if package_json else project_root).resolve()
   682→   682→+    payload = _read_package_json(package_json) if package_json else {}
   683→   683→+
   684→   684→+    deps = _dep_set(payload, "dependencies") | _dep_set(payload, "peerDependencies") | _dep_set(
   685→   685→+        payload, "optionalDependencies"
   686→   686→+    )
   687→   687→+    dev_deps = _dep_set(payload, "devDependencies")
   688→   688→+    scripts = _script_values(payload)
   689→   689→+

   697→   697→+            scripts=scripts,

   701→   701→+    ordered = sorted(candidates, key=lambda c: (-c.score, c.label))
   702→   702→+
   703→   703→+    primary_id: FrameworkId | None = None
   704→   704→+    primary_score = 0

   742→   742→@@ -0,0 +1,158 @@
   743→   743→+# Next.js Framework Module
   744→   744→+
   745→   745→+This document explains the Next.js framework support used by Desloppify's TypeScript and JavaScript plugins.
   746→   746→+
   747→   747→+It covers:
   748→   748→+
   749→   749→+- What the Next.js framework module does
   750→   750→+- How framework detection and scanning flow works
   751→   751→+- What each file in `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/nextjs/` is responsible for
   752→   752→+- Which shared files outside this folder affect behavior
   753→   753→+- Current limits and safe extension points
   754→   754→+
   755→   755→+If you are new to this code, start with the `detect_nextjs_framework_smells` section and then read the "Scan flow" section.
   756→   756→+
   757→   757→+## High-level purpose
   758→   758→+
   759→   759→+The Next.js framework module adds framework-aware smells that generic code-quality detectors do not catch.
   760→   760→+
   761→   761→+Current scope includes:
   762→   762→+
   763→   763→+- App Router vs Pages Router migration and misuse signals
   764→   764→+- Client/server boundary misuse (`"use client"`, `"use server"`, server-only imports/exports)
   765→   765→+- Route handler and middleware context misuse
   766→   766→+- Next.js API misuse in wrong router contexts
   767→   767→+- Environment variable leakage in client modules
   768→   768→+- `next lint` integration as a framework quality gate (`next_lint` detector)
   769→   769→+
   770→   770→+This module is intentionally heuristic-heavy (regex/file-structure based) so scans remain fast and robust without requiring full compiler semantics.
   771→   771→+
   772→   772→+## Module map
   773→   773→+
   774→   774→+Files in this folder:
   775→   775→+
   776→   776→+- `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/nextjs/__init__.py`
   777→   777→+- `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/nextjs/info.py`
   778→   778→+- `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/nextjs/phase.py`
   779→   779→+- `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/nextjs/scanners.py`
   780→   780→+
   781→   781→+### What each file does
   782→   782→+
   783→   783→+`__init__.py`:
   784→   784→+
   785→   785→+- Exposes the framework info contract and shared phase entrypoint for imports
   786→   786→+
   787→   787→+`info.py`:
   788→   788→+
   789→   789→+- Defines `NextjsFrameworkInfo`
   790→   790→+- Converts generic framework detection output into Next.js-specific router roots and flags
   791→   791→+
   792→   792→+`phase.py`:
   793→   793→+
   794→   794→+- Orchestrates all Next.js scanners
   795→   795→+- Converts scanner entries into normalized issues (`nextjs` detector)
   796→   796→+- Runs `next lint` integration and emits normalized lint issues (`next_lint` detector)
   797→   797→+
   798→   798→+`scanners.py`:
   799→   799→+
   800→   800→+- Implements all Next.js smell scanners
   801→   801→+- Performs fast source-file discovery and content heuristics
   802→   802→+- Returns normalized scanner entries for the phase orchestrator
   803→   803→+
   804→   804→+## Shared surfaces outside this folder
   805→   805→+
   806→   806→+These files are part of the same feature boundary and should be considered together:
   807→   807→+
   808→   808→+- `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/detect.py`
   809→   809→+- `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/catalog.py`
   810→   810→+- `desloppify/languages/typescript/frameworks/types.py`
   811→   811→+- `desloppify/languages/typescript/phases_smells.py`
   812→   812→+- `desloppify/languages/javascript/phases_nextjs.py`
   813→   813→+- `desloppify/languages/_framework/node/next_lint.py`
   814→   814→+- `desloppify/base/discovery/source.py`
   815→   815→+
   816→   816→+### Responsibility split
   817→   817→+
   818→   818→+- `frameworks/detect.py` decides whether Next.js is the primary framework and where package roots are.
   819→   819→+- `nextjs/info.py` exposes Next.js routing context (`app_roots`, `pages_roots`) used by scanners.
   820→   820→+- `nextjs/scanners.py` only finds smell candidates.
   821→   821→+- `nextjs/phase.py` maps candidates to detector issues and handles scoring potentials.
   822→   822→+- `next_lint.py` provides lint execution/parsing, independent of smell mapping.
   823→   823→+
   824→   824→+## Detectors
   825→   825→+
   826→   826→+This module emits findings under:
   827→   827→+
   828→   828→+- `nextjs`
   829→   829→+- `next_lint`
   830→   830→+
   831→   831→+Registry/scoring wiring lives outside this folder in:
   832→   832→+
   833→   833→+- `desloppify/base/registry/catalog_entries.py`
   834→   834→+- `desloppify/base/registry/catalog_models.py`
   835→   835→+- `desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py`
   836→   836→+
   837→   837→+## Scan flow in plain language
   838→   838→+
   839→   839→+When TypeScript or JavaScript code smells run for a Next.js project, flow is:
   840→   840→+
   841→   841→+1. Framework detection picks primary framework from package markers and scripts.
   842→   842→+2. Next.js info derives App/Pages router roots from detection evidence.
   843→   843→+3. Shared Next.js phase runs all scanner functions.
   844→   844→+4. Scanner entries are mapped into normalized `nextjs` issues.
   845→   845→+5. `next lint` is executed and mapped into `next_lint` issues.
   846→   846→+6. Potentials are returned for scoring and state merge.
   847→   847→+
   848→   848→+## `next lint` behavior
   849→   849→+
   850→   850→+`run_next_lint(...)` executes:
   851→   851→+
   852→   852→+- `npx --no-install next lint --format json`
   853→   853→+
   854→   854→+Behavior:
   855→   855→+
   856→   856→+- If lint runs and returns JSON, file-level lint findings are emitted.
   857→   857→+- If lint cannot run or output cannot be parsed, a `next_lint::unavailable` issue is emitted.
   858→   858→+
   859→   859→+This keeps lint participation explicit in Next.js scans instead of silently skipping.
   860→   860→+
   861→   861→+## Smell families covered
   862→   862→+
   863→   863→+Current high-value families include:
   864→   864→+
   865→   865→+- `"use client"` placement and missing directive checks
   866→   866→+- `"use server"` placement checks (module-level misuse only)
   867→   867→+- Server-only imports in client modules (`next/headers`, `next/server`, `next/cache`, `server-only`, Node built-ins)
   868→   868→+- Server-only Next exports from client modules (`metadata`, `generateMetadata`, `revalidate`, `dynamic`, etc.)
   869→   869→+- Pages Router APIs used under App Router (`getServerSideProps`, `getStaticProps`, etc.)
   870→   870→+- `next/navigation` usage in Pages Router files
   871→   871→+- App Router metadata/config exports in Pages Router files
   872→   872→+- Pages API route files exporting App Router route-handler HTTP functions
   873→   873→+- App Router route handler and middleware misuse
   874→   874→+- `next/head` usage in App Router
   875→   875→+- `next/document` imports outside valid `_document.*` pages context
   876→   876→+- Browser global usage in App Router modules missing `"use client"`
   877→   877→+- Client layout smell and async client component smell
   878→   878→+- Mixed `app/` and `pages/` router project smell
   879→   879→+- Env leakage in client modules via non-`NEXT_PUBLIC_*` `process.env` usage
   880→   880→+
   881→   881→+## Extending this module safely
   882→   882→+
   883→   883→+When adding a new smell:
   884→   884→+
   885→   885→+1. Add scanner logic in `scanners.py`.
   886→   886→+2. Return compact entries (`file`, `line`, and minimal structured detail).
   887→   887→+3. Map entries to `make_issue(...)` in `phase.py` with clear `id`, `summary`, and `detail`.
   888→   888→+4. Update/extend tests in:
   889→   889→+   - `desloppify/languages/typescript/tests/test_ts_nextjs_framework.py`
   890→   890→+   - `desloppify/languages/javascript/tests/test_js_nextjs_framework.py` (if JS parity applies)
   891→   891→+5. Keep logic shared (do not duplicate TS vs JS framework smell rules).
   892→   892→+
   893→   893→+## Limits and tradeoffs
   894→   894→+
   895→   895→+- Scanners are heuristic, not compiler-accurate.
   896→   896→+- Some patterns are intentionally conservative to avoid noisy false positives.
   897→   897→+- Router/middleware checks rely on conventional Next.js file placement.
   898→   898→+- `next lint` requires project dependencies to be present for full lint execution.
   899→   899→+
   900→   900→+These tradeoffs are deliberate: fast scans with high-signal framework smells, while preserving a clear extension path when stronger analysis is needed.

   972→   972→@@ -0,0 +1,48 @@
   973→   973→+"""Next.js framework info derived from generic TypeScript framework detection."""
   974→   974→+
   975→   975→+from __future__ import annotations
   976→   976→+
   977→   977→+from dataclasses import dataclass
   978→   978→+from pathlib import Path
   979→   979→+from typing import Any
   980→   980→+
   981→   981→+from desloppify.languages.typescript.frameworks.types import PrimaryFrameworkDetection
   982→   982→+
   983→   983→+
   984→   984→+@dataclass(frozen=True)
   985→   985→+class NextjsFrameworkInfo:
   986→   986→+    is_primary: bool
   987→   987→+    package_root: Path
   988→   988→+    package_json_relpath: str | None
   989→   989→+    app_roots: tuple[str, ...]
   990→   990→+    pages_roots: tuple[str, ...]
   991→   991→+
   992→   992→+    @property
   993→   993→+    def uses_app_router(self) -> bool:
   994→   994→+        return bool(self.app_roots)
   995→   995→+
   996→   996→+    @property
   997→   997→+    def uses_pages_router(self) -> bool:
   998→   998→+        return bool(self.pages_roots)
   999→   999→+
  1000→  1000→+
  1001→  1001→+def _tuple_str(value: Any) -> tuple[str, ...]:
  1002→  1002→+    if not isinstance(value, list):
  1003→  1003→+        return ()
  1004→  1004→+    return tuple(str(v) for v in value if isinstance(v, str))
  1005→  1005→+
  1006→  1006→+
  1007→  1007→+def nextjs_info_from_detection(result: PrimaryFrameworkDetection) -> NextjsFrameworkInfo:
  1008→  1008→+    candidate = next((c for c in result.candidates if c.id == "nextjs"), None)
  1009→  1009→+    evidence = candidate.evidence if candidate is not None else {}
  1010→  1010→+    return NextjsFrameworkInfo(
  1011→  1011→+        is_primary=(result.primary_id == "nextjs"),
  1012→  1012→+        package_root=result.package_root,
  1013→  1013→+        package_json_relpath=result.package_json_relpath,
  1014→  1014→+        app_roots=_tuple_str(evidence.get("app_roots")),
  1015→  1015→+        pages_roots=_tuple_str(evidence.get("pages_roots")),
  1016→  1016→+    )
  1017→  1017→+
  1018→  1018→+
  1019→  1019→+__all__ = ["NextjsFrameworkInfo", "nextjs_info_from_detection"]

  1067→  1067→+def detect_nextjs_framework_smells(
  1068→  1068→+    scan_root: Path,
  1069→  1069→+    info: NextjsFrameworkInfo,
  1070→  1070→+    log_fn: Callable[[str], None],
  1071→  1071→+) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
  1072→  1072→+    results: list[Issue] = []
  1073→  1073→+    nextjs_scanned = 0
  1074→  1074→+    next_lint_potential = 0
  1075→  1075→+
  1076→  1076→+    next_lint_entries, next_lint_potential, next_lint_error = run_next_lint(scan_root)
  1077→  1077→+    if next_lint_error:
  1078→  1078→+        results.append(
  1079→  1079→+            make_issue(
  1080→  1080→+                "next_lint",
  1081→  1081→+                info.package_json_relpath or "package.json",
  1082→  1082→+                "unavailable",
  1083→  1083→+                tier=2,
  1084→  1084→+                confidence="high",
  1085→  1085→+                summary=f"Next.js lint did not run: {next_lint_error}.",
  1086→  1086→+                detail={"line": 1, "error": next_lint_error},
  1087→  1087→+            )
  1088→  1088→+        )
  1089→  1089→+        next_lint_potential = max(next_lint_potential, 1)
  1090→  1090→+    else:
  1091→  1091→+        for entry in next_lint_entries:
  1092→  1092→+            results.append(
  1093→  1093→+                make_issue(
  1094→  1094→+                    "next_lint",
  1095→  1095→+                    entry["file"],
  1096→  1096→+                    "lint",
  1097→  1097→+                    tier=2,
  1098→  1098→+                    confidence="high",
  1099→  1099→+                    summary=(
  1100→  1100→+                        f"next lint: {entry['message']} ({entry.get('count', 1)} issue(s) in file)"
  1101→  1101→+                    ),
  1102→  1102→+                    detail={
  1103→  1103→+                        "line": entry.get("line", 1),
  1104→  1104→+                        "count": entry.get("count", 1),
  1105→  1105→+                        "messages": entry.get("messages", []),
  1106→  1106→+                    },
  1107→  1107→+                )
  1108→  1108→+            )
  1109→  1109→+    if next_lint_potential > 0:
  1110→  1110→+        log_fn(f"    next lint: {len(next_lint_entries)} file(s) with issues")
  1111→  1111→+
  1112→  1112→+    use_client_not_first_entries, scanned = scan_nextjs_use_client_not_first(scan_root, info)
  1113→  1113→+    nextjs_scanned = max(nextjs_scanned, scanned)
  1114→  1114→+    for entry in use_client_not_first_entries:
  1115→  1115→+        results.append(
  1116→  1116→+            make_issue(
  1117→  1117→+                "nextjs",
  1118→  1118→+                entry["file"],
  1119→  1119→+                "use_client_not_first",
  1120→  1120→+                tier=2,
  1121→  1121→+                confidence="high",
  1122→  1122→+                summary="'use client' directive is present but not the first meaningful line (invalid in Next.js).",
  1123→  1123→+                detail={"line": entry["line"]},
  1124→  1124→+            )
  1125→  1125→+        )
  1126→  1126→+    if use_client_not_first_entries:
  1127→  1127→+        log_fn(
  1128→  1128→+            "       nextjs: "
  1129→  1129→+            f"{len(use_client_not_first_entries)} App Router files contain a non-top-level 'use client' directive"
  1130→  1130→+        )
  1131→  1131→+
  1132→  1132→+    error_missing_client_entries, scanned = scan_nextjs_error_files_missing_use_client(scan_root, info)
  1133→  1133→+    nextjs_scanned = max(nextjs_scanned, scanned)
  1134→  1134→+    for entry in error_missing_client_entries:
  1135→  1135→+        results.append(
  1136→  1136→+            make_issue(
  1137→  1137→+                "nextjs",
  1138→  1138→+                entry["file"],
  1139→  1139→+                f"error_file_missing_use_client::{entry.get('name','error')}",
  1140→  1140→+                tier=2,
  1141→  1141→+                confidence="high",
  1142→  1142→+                summary="App Router error boundary module is missing 'use client' (required for error.js/error.tsx).",
  1143→  1143→+                detail={"line": entry["line"], "name": entry.get("name")},
  1144→  1144→+            )
  1145→  1145→+        )
  1146→  1146→+    if error_missing_client_entries:
  1147→  1147→+        log_fn(
  1148→  1148→+            "       nextjs: "
  1149→  1149→+            f"{len(error_missing_client_entries)} App Router error boundary files missing 'use client'"
  1150→  1150→+        )
  1151→  1151→+
  1152→  1152→+    pages_artifact_entries, scanned = scan_nextjs_pages_router_artifacts_in_app_router(scan_root, info)
  1153→  1153→+    nextjs_scanned = max(nextjs_scanned, scanned)
  1154→  1154→+    for entry in pages_artifact_entries:
  1155→  1155→+        results.append(
  1156→  1156→+            make_issue(
  1157→  1157→+                "nextjs",
  1158→  1158→+                entry["file"],
  1159→  1159→+                f"pages_router_artifact_in_app_router::{entry.get('name','artifact')}",
  1160→  1160→+                tier=3,
  1161→  1161→+                confidence="high",
  1162→  1162→+                summary=(
  1163→  1163→+                    f"App Router tree contains Pages Router artifact file {entry.get('name')} (likely migration artifact)."
  1164→  1164→+                ),
  1165→  1165→+                detail={"line": entry["line"], "name": entry.get("name")},
  1166→  1166→+            )
  1167→  1167→+        )
  1168→  1168→+    if pages_artifact_entries:
  1169→  1169→+        log_fn("       nextjs: " f"{len(pages_artifact_entries)} Pages Router artifact files found under app/")
  1170→  1170→+
  1171→  1171→+    missing_client_entries, scanned = scan_rsc_missing_use_client(scan_root, info)
  1172→  1172→+    nextjs_scanned = max(nextjs_scanned, scanned)
  1173→  1173→+    for entry in missing_client_entries:
  1174→  1174→+        results.append(
  1175→  1175→+            make_issue(
  1176→  1176→+                "nextjs",
  1177→  1177→+                entry["file"],
  1178→  1178→+                f"missing_use_client::{entry['hook']}",
  1179→  1179→+                tier=2,
  1180→  1180→+                confidence="medium",
  1181→  1181→+                summary=f"Missing 'use client' directive: App Router module uses {entry['hook']}()",
  1182→  1182→+                detail={"line": entry["line"], "hook": entry["hook"]},
  1183→  1183→+            )
  1184→  1184→+        )
  1185→  1185→+    if missing_client_entries:
  1186→  1186→+        log_fn("       nextjs: " f"{len(missing_client_entries)} App Router files missing 'use client'")
  1187→  1187→+
  1188→  1188→+    nav_hook_entries, scanned = scan_nextjs_navigation_hooks_missing_use_client(scan_root, info)
  1189→  1189→+    nextjs_scanned = max(nextjs_scanned, scanned)
  1190→  1190→+    for entry in nav_hook_entries:
  1191→  1191→+        results.append(
  1192→  1192→+            make_issue(

  1194→  1194→+                entry["file"],
  1195→  1195→+                f"nav_hook_missing_use_client::{entry['hook']}",
  1196→  1196→+                tier=2,
  1197→  1197→+                confidence="high",
  1198→  1198→+                summary=f"Missing 'use client' directive: App Router module uses {entry['hook']}()",
  1199→  1199→+                detail={"line": entry["line"], "hook": entry["hook"]},
```

```
   122→diff --git a/desloppify/languages/_framework/node/next_lint.py b/desloppify/languages/_framework/node/next_lint.py
   123→new file mode 100644
   124→index 00000000..cd2174ae
   125→--- /dev/null
   126→+++ b/desloppify/languages/_framework/node/next_lint.py
   127→@@ -0,0 +1,120 @@
   128→+"""Next.js `next lint` integration.
   129→+
   130→+This is treated as a framework-level smell source for Next.js projects. It
   131→+parses ESLint JSON output and maps it into per-file issues to keep state size
   132→+bounded.
   133→+"""
   134→+
   135→+from __future__ import annotations
   136→+
   137→+import json
   138→+import logging
   139→+import shutil
   140→+import subprocess  # nosec B404
   141→+from pathlib import Path
   142→+
   143→+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   144→+_proc_runtime = subprocess
   145→+
   146→+
   147→+def _extract_json_array(text: str) -> str | None:
   148→+    """Best-effort: return the first JSON array substring in *text*."""
   149→+    start = text.find("[")
   150→+    if start == -1:
   151→+        return None
   152→+    end = text.rfind("]")
   153→+    if end == -1 or end <= start:
   154→+        return None
   155→+    return text[start : end + 1]
   156→+
   157→+
   158→+def _relativize(filepath: str, project_root: Path) -> str:
   159→+    try:
   160→+        p = Path(filepath)
   161→+        if p.is_absolute():
   162→+            return str(p.resolve().relative_to(project_root.resolve()))
   163→+    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
   164→+        pass
   165→+    return filepath
   166→+
   167→+
   168→+def run_next_lint(project_root: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], int, str | None]:
   169→+    """Run `next lint --format json` and return (entries, potential, error).
   170→+
   171→+    - entries are *per-file* aggregates: {file, line, message, count, messages}
   172→+    - potential is the number of files lint reported on (len(json array))
   173→+    - error is a short string when lint could not be run/parsed
   174→+    """
   175→+    npx_path = shutil.which("npx")
   176→+    if not npx_path:
   177→+        return [], 0, "npx executable not found in PATH"
   178→+
   179→+    cmd = [npx_path, "--no-install", "next", "lint", "--format", "json"]
   180→+    try:
   181→+        result = _proc_runtime.run(  # nosec B603
   182→+            cmd,
   183→+            capture_output=True,
   184→+            text=True,
   185→+            cwd=project_root,
   186→+            timeout=180,
   187→+        )
   188→+    except (_proc_runtime.SubprocessError, OSError) as exc:
   189→+        return [], 0, f"failed to run next lint: {exc}"
   190→+
   191→+    raw = (result.stdout or "").strip()
   192→+    if not raw:
   193→+        raw = (result.stderr or "").strip()
   194→+    json_text = _extract_json_array(raw)
   195→+    if not json_text:
   196→+        return [], 0, "next lint did not return JSON output (try installing dependencies)"
   197→+
   198→+    try:
   199→+        data = json.loads(json_text)
   200→+    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
   201→+        logger.debug("next lint JSON parse failed: %s", exc)
   202→+        return [], 0, "could not parse next lint JSON output"
   203→+
   204→+    if not isinstance(data, list):
   205→+        return [], 0, "next lint JSON output was not a list"
   206→+
   207→+    potential = len(data)
   208→+    entries: list[dict] = []
   209→+    for fobj in data:
   210→+        if not isinstance(fobj, dict):
   211→+            continue
   212→+        file_path = fobj.get("filePath") or ""
   213→+        messages = fobj.get("messages") or []
   214→+        if not file_path or not isinstance(messages, list) or not messages:
   215→+            continue
   216→+
   217→+        rel = _relativize(str(file_path), project_root)
   218→+        first = next((m for m in messages if isinstance(m, dict)), None)
   219→+        if first is None:
   220→+            continue
   221→+        line = first.get("line") if isinstance(first.get("line"), int) else 1
   222→+        msg = first.get("message") if isinstance(first.get("message"), str) else "Lint issue"
   223→+        entries.append(
   224→+            {
   225→+                "file": rel,
   226→+                "line": line if line and line > 0 else 1,
   227→+                "message": msg,
   228→+                "count": len(messages),
   229→+                "messages": [
   230→+                    {
   231→+                        "line": m.get("line", 0),
   232→+                        "column": m.get("column", 0),
   233→+                        "ruleId": m.get("ruleId", ""),
   234→+                        "message": m.get("message", ""),
   235→+                        "severity": m.get("severity", 0),
   236→+                    }
   237→+                    for m in messages
   238→+                    if isinstance(m, dict)
   239→+                ][:50],
   240→+            }
   241→+        )
   242→+
   243→+    return entries, potential, None
   244→+
   245→+
   246→+__all__ = ["run_next_lint"]
   247→+
```
