```
1→"""Basic TypeScript detector phase runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
    10→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import adjust_potential
    11→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangRuntimeContract
    12→from desloppify.languages._framework.issue_factories import make_unused_issues
    13→import desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors.deprecated as deprecated_detector_mod
    14→import desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors.exports as exports_detector_mod
    15→import desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors.logs as logs_detector_mod
    16→import desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors.unused as unused_detector_mod
    17→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    18→
    19→
    20→def phase_logs(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    21→    log_result = logs_detector_mod.detect_logs(path)
    22→    log_entries = log_result.entries
    23→    total_files = log_result.population_size
    24→    log_groups: dict[tuple, list] = defaultdict(list)
    25→    for entry in log_entries:
    26→        log_groups[(entry["file"], entry["tag"])].append(entry)
    27→
    28→    results = []
    29→    for (file, tag), entries in log_groups.items():
    30→        results.append(
    31→            make_issue(
    32→                "logs",
    33→                file,
    34→                tag,
    35→                tier=1,
    36→                confidence="high",
    37→                summary=f"{len(entries)} tagged logs [{tag}]",
    38→                detail={
    39→                    "count": len(entries),
    40→                    "lines": [entry["line"] for entry in entries[:20]],
    41→                },
    42→            )
    43→        )
    44→    log(f"         {len(log_entries)} instances → {len(results)} issues")
    45→    return results, {"logs": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files)}
    46→
    47→
    48→def phase_unused(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    49→    entries, total_files = unused_detector_mod.detect_unused(path)
    50→    return make_unused_issues(entries, log), {
    51→        "unused": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
    52→    }
    53→
    54→
    55→def phase_exports(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    56→    export_entries, total_exports = exports_detector_mod.detect_dead_exports(path)
    57→    results = []
    58→    for entry in export_entries:
    59→        results.append(
    60→            make_issue(
    61→                "exports",
    62→                entry["file"],
    63→                entry["name"],
    64→                tier=2,
    65→                confidence="high",
    66→                summary=f"Dead export: {entry['name']}",
    67→                detail={"line": entry.get("line"), "kind": entry.get("kind")},
    68→            )
    69→        )
    70→    log(f"         {len(export_entries)} instances → {len(results)} issues")
    71→    return results, {"exports": total_exports}
    72→
    73→
    74→def phase_deprecated(
    75→    path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract
    76→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    77→    dep_result = deprecated_detector_mod.detect_deprecated_result(path)
    78→    dep_entries = dep_result.entries
    79→    total_deprecated = dep_result.population_size
    80→    results = []
    81→    for entry in dep_entries:
    82→        if entry["kind"] == "property":
    83→            continue
    84→        tier = 1 if entry["importers"] == 0 else 3
    85→        results.append(
    86→            make_issue(
    87→                "deprecated",
    88→                entry["file"],
    89→                entry["symbol"],
    90→                tier=tier,
    91→                confidence="high",
    92→                summary=f"Deprecated: {entry['symbol']} ({entry['importers']} importers)"
    93→                + (" → safe to delete" if entry["importers"] == 0 else ""),
    94→                detail={"importers": entry["importers"], "line": entry["line"]},
    95→            )
    96→        )
    97→    log(
    98→        f"         {len(dep_entries)} instances → {len(results)} issues (properties suppressed)"
    99→    )
   100→    return results, {"deprecated": total_deprecated}
```

```
1→"""Shared language-plugin registration helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import inspect
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import TypeVar
     8→
     9→from . import state
    10→from ..base.types import LangConfig
    11→from .resolution import make_lang_config
    12→from ..structure_validation import validate_lang_structure
    13→
    14→ConfigType = TypeVar("ConfigType")
    15→
    16→
    17→def register_lang_class(name: str, config_cls: type[ConfigType]) -> None:
    18→    """Register one language class through canonical framework validation flow."""
    19→    register_lang_structure_fn = validate_lang_structure
    20→    module = inspect.getmodule(config_cls)
    21→    if module and hasattr(module, "__file__"):
    22→        register_lang_structure_fn(Path(module.__file__).parent, name)
    23→    if isinstance(config_cls, type) and issubclass(config_cls, LangConfig):
    24→        cfg = make_lang_config(name, config_cls)
    25→        state.register(name, cfg)
    26→        return
    27→    state.register(name, config_cls)
    28→
    29→
    30→def register_lang_class_with(
    31→    name: str,
    32→    config_cls: type[ConfigType],
    33→    *,
    34→    validate_lang_structure_fn=validate_lang_structure,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    """Register a class with injectable structure validation seam (tests/callers)."""
    37→    module = inspect.getmodule(config_cls)
    38→    if module and hasattr(module, "__file__"):
    39→        validate_lang_structure_fn(Path(module.__file__).parent, name)
    40→    if isinstance(config_cls, type) and issubclass(config_cls, LangConfig):
    41→        cfg = make_lang_config(name, config_cls)
    42→        state.register(name, cfg)
    43→        return
    44→    state.register(name, config_cls)
    45→
    46→
    47→def register_full_plugin(
    48→    name: str,
    49→    config_cls: type[ConfigType],
    50→    *,
    51→    test_coverage: object,
    52→) -> None:
    53→    """Register a full language plugin with uniform hooks + duplicate guard."""
    54→    state.register_lang_hooks(name, test_coverage=test_coverage)
    55→    if state.is_registered(name):
    56→        return
    57→    register_lang_class(name, config_cls)
    58→
    59→
    60→__all__ = [
    61→    "register_full_plugin",
    62→    "register_lang_class",
    63→    "register_lang_class_with",
    64→]
    65→
```

```
1→"""Language registration API focused on canonical framework boundaries.
     2→
     3→Runtime code should prefer ``desloppify.languages.framework`` for framework
     4→access; this module stays limited to registration and language lookup.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Callable
    10→from typing import TypeVar
    11→
    12→from desloppify.languages.framework import (
    13→    LangConfig,
    14→    auto_detect_lang,
    15→    available_langs,
    16→    get_lang,
    17→    make_lang_config,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.languages._framework.contract_validation import validate_lang_contract
    20→from desloppify.languages._framework.policy import REQUIRED_DIRS, REQUIRED_FILES
    21→from desloppify.languages._framework.registry.discovery import load_all
    22→from desloppify.languages._framework.registry.registration import (
    23→    register_lang_class_with,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.languages._framework.registry.state import all_keys, register
    26→from desloppify.languages._framework.structure_validation import validate_lang_structure
    27→
    28→T = TypeVar("T")
    29→
    30→
    31→def register_lang(name: str) -> Callable[[T], T]:
    32→    """Decorator to register a language config class.
    33→
    34→    Validates structure, instantiates the class, validates the contract,
    35→    and stores the *instance* in the registry.
    36→    """
    37→
    38→    def decorator(cls: T) -> T:
    39→        register_lang_class_with(
    40→            name,
    41→            cls,
    42→            validate_lang_structure_fn=validate_lang_structure,
    43→        )
    44→        return cls
    45→
    46→    return decorator
    47→
    48→
    49→def register_generic_lang(name: str, cfg: LangConfig) -> None:
    50→    """Register a pre-built language plugin instance (no package structure required)."""
    51→    validate_lang_contract(name, cfg)
    52→    register(name, cfg)
    53→
    54→
    55→def reload_lang_plugins() -> list[str]:
    56→    """Force plugin rediscovery and return refreshed language names."""
    57→    load_all(force_reload=True)
    58→    return sorted(all_keys())
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = [
    62→    "REQUIRED_FILES",
    63→    "REQUIRED_DIRS",
    64→    "register_lang",
    65→    "register_generic_lang",
    66→    "reload_lang_plugins",
    67→    "get_lang",
    68→    "available_langs",
    69→    "auto_detect_lang",
    70→    "make_lang_config",
    71→    "validate_lang_structure",
    72→    "validate_lang_contract",
    73→]
    74→
```

```
1→"""Tree-sitter phase factories for language plugins.
     2→
     3→Each factory takes a ``TreeSitterLangSpec`` and returns a ``DetectorPhase``.
     4→All phases resolve ``lang.file_finder`` at runtime.
     5→
     6→Used by both generic plugins (via ``generic.py``) and full plugins (C#, Dart,
     7→GDScript, TypeScript) that want tree-sitter-powered detection without
     8→duplicating the phase construction logic.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
    18→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.smell_contracts import normalize_smell_matches
    19→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase
    20→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    21→
    22→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    23→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangRuntimeContract
    24→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import TreeSitterLangSpec
    25→
    26→# ── Phase factories ────────────────────────────────────────
    27→
    28→
    29→def make_ast_smells_phase(spec: TreeSitterLangSpec) -> DetectorPhase:
    30→    """Create an AST smells phase: empty catches + unreachable code."""
    31→
    32→    def run(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    33→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.smells import (
    34→            detect_empty_catches,
    35→            detect_unreachable_code,
    36→        )
    37→
    38→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
    39→        issues: list[Issue] = []
    40→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    41→
    42→        catches = normalize_smell_matches(
    43→            detect_empty_catches(file_list, spec),
    44→            content_key="type",
    45→            default_content="catch",
    46→        )
    47→        for match in catches:
    48→            issues.append(make_issue(
    49→                "smells", match.file, f"empty_catch::{match.line}",
    50→                tier=3, confidence="high",
    51→                summary=f"Empty {match.content} — swallows errors silently",
    52→            ))
    53→        if catches:
    54→            potentials["empty_catch"] = len(catches)
    55→            log(f"         empty catch blocks: {len(catches)}")
    56→
    57→        unreachable = normalize_smell_matches(
    58→            detect_unreachable_code(file_list, spec),
    59→            content_key="after",
    60→            default_content="return",
    61→        )
    62→        for match in unreachable:
    63→            issues.append(make_issue(
    64→                "smells", match.file, f"unreachable_code::{match.line}",
    65→                tier=3, confidence="high",
    66→                summary=f"Unreachable code after {match.content}",
    67→            ))
    68→        if unreachable:
    69→            potentials["unreachable_code"] = len(unreachable)
    70→            log(f"         unreachable code: {len(unreachable)}")
    71→
    72→        return issues, potentials
    73→
    74→    return DetectorPhase("AST smells", run)
    75→
    76→
    77→def make_cohesion_phase(spec: TreeSitterLangSpec) -> DetectorPhase:
    78→    """Create a responsibility cohesion phase."""
    79→
    80→    def run(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    81→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.cohesion import (
    82→            detect_responsibility_cohesion,
    83→        )
    84→
    85→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
    86→        issues: list[Issue] = []
    87→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    88→
    89→        entries, checked = detect_responsibility_cohesion(file_list, spec)
    90→        for e in entries:
    91→            families = ", ".join(e["families"][:4])
    92→            issues.append(make_issue(
    93→                "responsibility_cohesion", e["file"],
    94→                f"cohesion::{e['file']}",
    95→                tier=3, confidence="medium",
    96→                summary=(
    97→                    f"{e['component_count']} disconnected function clusters "
    98→                    f"({e['function_count']} functions) — likely mixed responsibilities"
    99→                ),
   100→                detail={
   101→                    "cluster_count": e["component_count"],
   102→                    "family": families,
   103→                    "families": e["families"],
   104→                },
   105→            ))
   106→        if entries:
   107→            potentials["responsibility_cohesion"] = len(entries)
   108→            log(f"         low-cohesion files: {len(entries)}")
   109→
   110→        return issues, potentials
   111→
   112→    return DetectorPhase("Responsibility cohesion", run)
   113→
   114→
   115→def make_unused_imports_phase(spec: TreeSitterLangSpec) -> DetectorPhase:
   116→    """Create an unused imports phase."""
   117→
   118→    def run(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   119→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter.analysis.unused_imports import (
   120→            detect_unused_imports,
   121→        )
   122→
   123→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
   124→        issues: list[Issue] = []
   125→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
   126→
   127→        entries = detect_unused_imports(file_list, spec)
   128→        for e in entries:
   129→            issues.append(make_issue(
   130→                "unused", e["file"], f"unused_import::{e['line']}",
   131→                tier=3, confidence="medium",
   132→                summary=f"Unused import: {e['name']}",
   133→            ))
   134→        if entries:
   135→            potentials["unused_imports"] = len(entries)
   136→            log(f"         unused imports: {len(entries)}")
   137→
   138→        return issues, potentials
   139→
   140→    return DetectorPhase("Unused imports", run)
   141→
   142→
   143→# ── Convenience: all tree-sitter phases for a named language ──
   144→
   145→
   146→def all_treesitter_phases(spec_name: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
   147→    """Return all tree-sitter-powered phases for a language plugin.
   148→
   149→    Convenience bundle — returns AST smells, cohesion, and (when import
   150→    query exists) unused imports.  Returns [] if tree-sitter-language-pack
```

```
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from collections.abc import Callable

     9→
    10→
    11→CoverageStatus = Literal["full", "reduced"]
    12→
    13→
    14→class DetectorCoverageRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    """Persisted detector-level coverage confidence metadata."""
    16→
    17→    detector: str
    18→    status: CoverageStatus
    19→    confidence: float
    20→    summary: str
    21→    impact: str
    22→    remediation: str
    23→    tool: str
    24→    reason: str
    25→

    27→class DetectorEntry(TypedDict):

    29→
    30→    file: str
    31→    tier: int
    32→    confidence: str
    33→    summary: str

    37→
    38→
    39→class ScanCoverageRecord(TypedDict, total=False):

    42→    status: CoverageStatus

    48→
```

## Language Plugin Architecture Analysis

Based on my exploration of the desloppify codebase, here's the comprehensive architecture breakdown:

### 1. **Shared Framework Infrastructure (`_framework/`)**

The `_framework/` directory is the central hub for all plugin capabilities:

**Core Layers:**
- **`base/`** — Foundational shared types and helpers
  - `types.py`: Core TypedDicts and dataclasses: `DetectorPhase`, `LangConfig`, `LangRuntimeContract`, `DetectorEntry`, `FixerConfig`, `BoundaryRule`
  - `types_shared.py`: Shared data shapes for cross-framework boundaries
  - `phase_builders.py`: Factories for shared phases (`detector_phase_security()`, `detector_phase_test_coverage()`, `detector_phase_signature()`, etc.)
  - `shared_phases.py` / `shared_phases_structural.py` / `shared_phases_review.py`: Implementation of phase runners reused by all languages

- **`treesitter/`** — Tree-sitter analysis library (25+ modules)
  - `phases.py`: Phase factories (`make_ast_smells_phase()`, `make_cohesion_phase()`, `make_unused_imports_phase()`, `all_treesitter_phases()`)
  - `analysis/`: Complex analysis modules (complexity, cohesion, unused imports, smells)
  - `imports/`: Import graph construction and resolution
  - Specs for compiled/functional/scripting language classes

- **`generic_support/`** — Tool-agnostic language plugin system
  - `core.py`: `generic_lang()` function — registers tool-based languages (JS, Nim, OCaml, etc.) from tool specs
  - `capabilities.py`: Shared phase labels and zone rules
  - `registration.py`: Phase building and fixer registration

- **`registry/`** — Plugin discovery and registration
  - `registration.py`: `register_lang_class()`, `register_full_plugin()` 
  - `discovery.py`: Runtime plugin loading
  - `resolution.py`: Language config instantiation
  - `state.py`: In-memory registry of registered languages

### 2. **Language Plugin Structure Patterns**

**Three Architecture Patterns:**

#### **Pattern A: Full Class-Based (TypeScript, Go, Rust, C++, C#)**
```
desloppify/languages/{lang}/
├── __init__.py                 # LangConfig subclass + register() + register_hooks()
├── phases_*.py                 # DetectorPhase runners (language-specific)
├── detectors/                  # Detection modules
│   ├── deps.py                 # build_dep_graph() for coupling/orphan/cycle detection
│   ├── smells.py               # Detector implementations
│   └── ...
├── extractors.py               # FunctionExtractor for duplicate detection
├── _fixers.py                  # get_{lang}_fixers() -> dict[str, FixerConfig]
├── commands.py                 # get_detect_commands() for CLI
├── review.py                   # Module patterns, API surface, review guidance
├── _zones.py                   # Zone classification rules
├── test_coverage.py            # Test coverage hooks
└── tests/                      # Comprehensive test suite
```

**Key Files in TypeScript Example:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/__init__.py` — Instantiates `TypeScriptConfig(LangConfig)` with 13 detector phases
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases_basic.py` — `phase_logs()`, `phase_unused()`, `phase_exports()`, `phase_deprecated()`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases_structural.py` — Structural analysis phase
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases_coupling.py` — Coupling detection phase
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases_smells.py` — React-specific + general code smell detection

#### **Pattern B: Generic Tool-Based (JavaScript, Nim, OCaml, Zig, Bash, etc.)**
```
desloppify/languages/{lang}/
├── __init__.py                 # Single call to generic_lang(...) with tool specs
```

**Example:**
```python
# desloppify/languages/javascript/__init__.py
from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_support.core import generic_lang

generic_lang(
    name="javascript",
    extensions=[".js", ".jsx", ".mjs", ".cjs"],
    tools=[
        {
            "label": "ESLint",
            "cmd": "npx eslint . --format json --no-error-on-unmatched-pattern 2>/dev/null",
            "fmt": "eslint",
            "id": "eslint_warning",
            "tier": 2,
            "fix_cmd": "...",
        },
    ],
    exclude=["node_modules", "dist", "build"],
    depth="shallow",
    treesitter_spec=JS_SPEC,  # Optional: adds function extraction, cohesion analysis
)
```

#### **Pattern C: Minimal Stubs (Bash, Clojure, Elixir, Erlang, etc.)**
```
desloppify/languages/{lang}/
└── __init__.py                 # Empty or minimal placeholder
```

### 3. **DetectorPhase Architecture**

**`DetectorPhase` Definition:**
```python
@dataclass
class DetectorPhase:
    label: str  # e.g. "Logs", "Structural analysis"
    run: Callable[[Path, LangRuntimeContract], tuple[list[DetectorEntry], dict[str, int]]]
    slow: bool = False
```

**Signature Contract:**
- **Input**: `path` (root to scan), `lang` (runtime interface with settings, caches, dep graph, zone map)
- **Output**: Tuple of (issues list, potential dict)
- `DetectorEntry` (TypedDict): `{file, tier, confidence, summary, line?, detail?, name?}`
- Potential dict: Maps detector names to file/population counts for scoring

**Phase Ordering in TypeScript (13 phases):**
1. Logs — detects tagged console/logger statements
2. Unused (tsc) — TypeScript compiler unused symbols
3. Dead exports — unused exports
4. Deprecated — deprecated APIs
5. Structural analysis — file size, complexity, cyclomatic
6. Coupling + single-use + patterns + naming — dep analysis
7. Tree-sitter cohesion — responsibility cohesion (when available)
8. Signature analysis — function signature issues (shared framework)
9. Test coverage — test file discovery (shared framework)
10. Code smells — empty catches, unreachable code, React patterns
11. Security — vulnerability detection (shared framework)
12. Subjective review — code review data (shared framework)
13. Boilerplate duplication → Duplicates — code cloning detection (shared framework)

### 4. **LangConfig Structure**

**Core Contract (`types.py:99-251`):**
```python
@dataclass
class LangConfig:
    name: str
    extensions: list[str]  # [".ts", ".tsx"]
    exclusions: list[str]  # ["node_modules", ".d.ts"]
    default_src: str  # "src"
    
    # Dep graph builder (language-specific import parsing)
    build_dep_graph: DepGraphBuilder
    
    # Entry points (not orphaned even with 0 importers)
    entry_patterns: list[str]
    barrel_names: set[str]
    
    # Detector phases (ordered)
    phases: list[DetectorPhase]
    
    # Fixer registry
    fixers: dict[str, FixerConfig]
    
    # Area classification, functions extractor, boundaries, thresholds, zones
    # Review guidance, test coverage hooks, migration patterns, etc.
```

### 5. **Shared Phases Framework**

**Factory Pattern in `base/phase_builders.py`:**
```python
SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES = {
    "test_coverage": _make_detector_phase_factory(...),
    "security": _make_detector_phase_factory(...),
    "signature": _make_detector_phase_factory(...),
    "subjective_review": _make_detector_phase_factory(...),
    "duplicates": _make_detector_phase_factory(...),
}

detector_phase_test_coverage()  # Returns DetectorPhase
detector_phase_security()       # Returns DetectorPhase
```

**Shared Phase Implementations:**
- `phase_test_coverage()` — Detects test coverage gaps
- `phase_security()` — Default security checks (overridable per language)
- `phase_signature()` — Function signature issues
- `phase_subjective_review()` — Code review data
- `phase_dupes()` — Duplicate detection (slow)
- `shared_subjective_duplicates_tail()` — Convenience wrapper: [subjective, duplicates]

**Tree-sitter Phases:**
- `make_ast_smells_phase(spec)` — Empty catches, unreachable code
- `make_cohesion_phase(spec)` — Responsibility cohesion analysis
- `make_unused_imports_phase(spec)` — Unused import detection
- `all_treesitter_phases(spec_name)` — Bundle all for a language

### 6. **Cross-Language Sharing Patterns**

**Current State: NO direct cross-language plugin imports**

✓ All language plugins import from `_framework/` only (not from other language plugins)  
✗ JavaScript does NOT import from TypeScript  
✗ Rust does NOT import from Go  

**Example Import Patterns:**
```python
# TypeScript plugin (full)
from desloppify.languages._framework.base.phase_builders import (
    detector_phase_security,
    detector_phase_test_coverage,
)
from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, LangConfig
from desloppify.languages.typescript.phases_basic import phase_logs  # Own phases
from desloppify.languages.typescript.detectors.deps import build_dep_graph  # Own detectors

# JavaScript plugin (generic)
from desloppify.languages._framework.generic_support.core import generic_lang  # Framework only
from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import JS_SPEC
```

### 7. **Registration Pattern**

```python
# Full plugin registration
def register() -> None:
    register_full_plugin(
        "typescript",
        TypeScriptConfig,  # LangConfig subclass
        test_coverage=ts_test_coverage_hooks,
    )

# Generic plugin registration (no ceremony)
generic_lang(
    name="javascript",
    extensions=[".js", ".jsx"],
    tools=[...],
)
```

### 8. **Key Shared Abstractions**

| Abstraction | Location | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `DetectorPhase` | `base/types.py` | Phase contract: run function + label |
| `LangConfig` | `base/types.py` | Language configuration blueprint |
| `LangRuntimeContract` | `base/types.py` | Runtime interface (dep graph, zone map, settings) |
| `DetectorEntry` | `base/types_shared.py` | Normalized issue shape |
| `FixerConfig` | `base/types_shared.py` | Auto-fixer configuration |
| Phase builders | `base/phase_builders.py` | Shared phase factories |
| Tree-sitter phases | `treesitter/phases.py` | Language-agnostic AST analysis phases |
| Generic lang system | `generic_support/core.py` | Tool-spec-driven plugin factory |

### 9. **Framework Directory Structure Summary**

```
_framework/
├── base/                          # Core types, shared phases, contracts
│   ├── types.py                  # DetectorPhase, LangConfig, LangRuntimeContract
│   ├── types_shared.py           # DetectorEntry, FixerConfig, boundaries
│   ├── phase_builders.py         # Shared phase factories
│   ├── shared_phases.py          # Phase implementations (test_coverage, security, etc.)
│   ├── shared_phases_structural.py
│   ├── shared_phases_review.py
│   ├── smell_contracts.py        # Smell normalization
│   ├── lang_config_runtime.py    # Runtime settings management
│   └── structural.py
├── treesitter/                    # Tree-sitter framework (25 modules)
│   ├── phases.py                 # Phase factories
│   ├── analysis/                 # Cohesion, complexity, smells, unused imports
│   ├── imports/                  # Import graph, resolution, caching
│   ├── specs/                    # Language-specific AST specs
│   └── types.py
├── generic_support/               # Generic tool-based language system
│   ├── core.py                   # generic_lang() entry point
│   ├── registration.py           # Plugin building/registration
│   ├── capabilities.py           # Shared labels and zone rules
│   └── ...
├── generic_parts/                 # Tool runners and parsers
│   ├── tool_factories.py
│   ├── tool_spec.py
│   ├── tool_runner.py
│   ├── parsers.py                # ESLint, golangci, cargo, GNU, rubocop formats
│   └── ...
├── registry/                      # Plugin lifecycle
│   ├── registration.py           # register_lang_class, register_full_plugin
│   ├── discovery.py              # Plugin auto-discovery
│   ├── resolution.py             # Config instantiation
│   └── state.py                  # In-memory registry
├── commands/                      # Command scaffolding
├── runtime_support/               # Runtime accessors
├── review_data/                   # Review metadata
├── issue_factories.py            # Issue normalization helpers
├── facade_common.py
├── policy.py                      # Plugin structure requirements
├── scope.py
├── scoped_store.py
├── structure_validation.py        # Plugin package structure validation
└── contract_validation.py         # LangConfig contract validation
```

### 10. **Key Takeaways for PR #414 Framework Detection**

**The Pattern:**
- Framework detection should live in `_framework/` NOT in a language plugin
- If framework detection is language-specific, it should be in the individual plugin (e.g., `typescript/frameworks/` detecting React/Next.js)
- If framework detection is truly cross-language, it should be in `_framework/generic_support/` or a new `_framework/frameworks/` subpackage

**Existing Precedents:**
- TypeScript has React-specific detectors in `typescript/detectors/react/` (state sync, context nesting, hook bloat)
- These are imported only by the TS phase runner, never by other languages
- Tree-sitter phases are generic and reused (no framework assumptions)

**Recommendation:**
Framework detection should follow language-specific pattern (option 1) unless it's truly language-agnostic analysis. Put shared framework logic in `_framework/` and language-specific detectors in `{lang}/detectors/`.

```
boundary-triggered reconciliation pipeline that runs all sync steps
sequentially. Add execution_status (active/review) flags to clusters,
consolidate plan load/recovery into persistence module, and rename
reconcile modules for clarity.
```

```
+"""Shared boundary-triggered plan reconciliation pipeline."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+
+from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot

+from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import sync_triage_needed
+from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.workflow import (
+    ScoreSnapshot,
+    sync_communicate_score_needed,
+    sync_create_plan_needed,
+)
+
+
+@dataclass
+class ReconcileResult:
+    """Mutation summary for one boundary-triggered reconcile pass."""
+
+    subjective: QueueSyncResult | None = None
+    auto_cluster_changes: int = 0
+    communicate_score: QueueSyncResult | None = None
+    create_plan: QueueSyncResult | None = None
+    triage: QueueSyncResult | None = None
+    lifecycle_phase: str = ""
+    lifecycle_phase_changed: bool = False
+
+    @property
+    def dirty(self) -> bool:
+        return any(
+            (
+                self.subjective is not None and bool(self.subjective.changes),
+                self.auto_cluster_changes > 0,
+                self.communicate_score is not None
```

```
diff --git a/desloppify/engine/_plan/cluster_semantics.py b/desloppify/engine/_plan/cluster_semantics.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..2fc90a96
--- /dev/null
+++ b/desloppify/engine/_plan/cluster_semantics.py
@@ -0,0 +1,157 @@
+"""Canonical semantic helpers for plan clusters.
+
+Cluster behavior should be derived from explicit semantic metadata rather than
+from command-string sniffing spread across queue assembly and rendering code.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections.abc import MutableMapping
+from typing import Any
+
+from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
+
+ACTION_TYPE_AUTO_FIX = "auto_fix"
+ACTION_TYPE_REFACTOR = "refactor"
+ACTION_TYPE_MANUAL_FIX = "manual_fix"
+ACTION_TYPE_REORGANIZE = "reorganize"
+VALID_ACTION_TYPES = frozenset(
+    {
+        ACTION_TYPE_AUTO_FIX,
+        ACTION_TYPE_REFACTOR,
+        ACTION_TYPE_MANUAL_FIX,
+        ACTION_TYPE_REORGANIZE,
+    }
+)
+
+EXECUTION_POLICY_EPHEMERAL_AUTOPROMOTE = "ephemeral_autopromote"
+EXECUTION_POLICY_PLANNED_ONLY = "planned_only"
+VALID_EXECUTION_POLICIES = frozenset(
+    {
+        EXECUTION_POLICY_EPHEMERAL_AUTOPROMOTE,
+        EXECUTION_POLICY_PLANNED_ONLY,
+    }
+)
+
+
+def _action_text(cluster: MutableMapping[str, Any] | dict[str, Any]) -> str:
+    return str(cluster.get("action") or "").strip()
+
+
+def infer_cluster_action_type(
+    cluster: MutableMapping[str, Any] | dict[str, Any],
+    *,
+    detector: str = "",
+) -> str:
+    """Return the canonical action type for a cluster."""
+    explicit = str(cluster.get("action_type") or "").strip()
+    if explicit in VALID_ACTION_TYPES:
+        return explicit
+
+    action = _action_text(cluster)
+    if action.startswith("desloppify autofix ") and "--dry-run" in action:
+        return ACTION_TYPE_AUTO_FIX
+    if action == "desloppify move":
+        return ACTION_TYPE_REORGANIZE
+
+    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
+    if meta is not None:
+        # Legacy safeguard: a detector may be auto-fixable in principle, but
+        # if the stored action is not an autofix command, keep cluster handling
+        # conservative and render it as refactor work.
```

> AGENT

Here is the comprehensive analysis of all changes between v0.9.8 and HEAD on the 0.9.9 branch.

---

## Features (user-facing new capabilities)

### 1. Auto-resolve stale issues for deleted files (Fix #412)
**Commits:** `99a8228a`, `553174cc`

When a scan runs and a previously-flagged file no longer exists on disk, its open issues are now automatically resolved with status `auto_resolved` and a note "source file no longer exists." Previously, issues for deleted files would remain open and clutter the work queue. This uses a new `project_root` parameter on `verify_disappeared()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/engine/_state/merge_issues.py`.

### 2. Sequential reconciliation pipeline with execution status flags
**Commit:** `6bce1ccb`

Fixes a cluster tracker race condition on parallel updates. A new shared boundary-triggered reconciliation pipeline (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`) runs all sync steps sequentially: subjective dimensions, auto-clustering, communicate-score, create-plan, triage, and lifecycle phase. Clusters now carry `execution_status` flags (active/review). Plan load/recovery logic was consolidated into the persistence module.

### 3. Cluster semantics module and queue ownership
**Commit:** `6bfca003`

New `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/engine/_plan/cluster_semantics.py` provides canonical semantic helpers for plan clusters. Clusters now have explicit `action_type` (auto_fix, refactor, manual_fix, reorganize) and `execution_policy` (ephemeral_autopromote, planned_only) rather than relying on command-string sniffing. Work queue snapshot and plan ordering updated for lifecycle phase isolation.

### 4. Substantive work-product descriptions in triage attestations
**Commit:** `a9cf466b`

Triage attestation validation for organize/enrich/sense-check stages no longer requires literal cluster name references. Users can now provide detailed descriptions of the work they verified as an alternative. This makes the triage workflow less rigid.

### 5. Triage and issue semantics overhaul
**Commit:** `553174cc`

Major expansion of issue semantics (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/engine/_state/issue_semantics.py` grew by ~184 lines) and triage prompt system (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/prompt.py` grew by ~240 lines). Adds queue reorder capabilities and richer triage stage prompts/validation.

### 6. Reorganized cluster/override into subpackages
**Commit:** `9dd5a430`

- `cluster_ops_*` and `cluster_update` modules moved into `cluster/` subpackage
- `override_*` modules moved into `override/` subpackage
- New dynamic loaders module (`helpers/dynamic_loaders.py`)
- New scan preflight checks (`scan/preflight.py`)
- Triage completion policy and stage validation significantly enhanced
- Review batch execution phases restructured
- Tweet release script expanded with tests

### 7. Sense-check orchestration for triage
**Commit:** `99a8228a`

New orchestrator for the sense-check triage stage (`orchestrator_codex_sense.py` grew by ~91 lines), with expanded sense-check prompts and evidence parsing.

### 8. Plan phase cleanup support
**Commit:** `99a8228a`

New `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/phase_cleanup.py` and scan issue reconcile module for better plan lifecycle management.

---

## Bug Fixes (user-facing)

### 1. Unblock objective resolves while triage is pending
**Commit:** `c08b84de` -- **Author: Andrew Robinson (community contribution)**

Previously, when triage was stale (new review items arrived), the triage guardrail could block `resolve` commands even for objective work unrelated to review. Now, if there's an objective backlog and the pending items are review-only, the guardrail shows an informational message instead of blocking. The message changes from "not yet triaged, run triage" to "they will activate after the current objective backlog is clear."

### 2. Improve C/C++ detector scoping (PR #415)
**Commit:** `7fe01b42` -- **Co-authored with Dragoy (community contribution)**

- Security findings now filtered to scoped first-party file set (avoids flagging vendor/third-party C++ code)
- CMake-based test coverage mapping via `add_executable`/`add_library`/`target_sources`
- Disabled unsound generic unused-import phase for C++
- Fixed `_extract_import_name` for C++ header extensions (.h, .hh, .hpp)

### 3. Restore push triggers on PyPI publish workflow
**Commit:** `b3e979bc`

CI contract test expected `push.tags` and `push.branches` triggers on the publish workflow. Restored them while keeping the release gate for main.

### 4. Stub requests module in tweet release tests
**Commit:** `47f2e0e5`

Fixed test isolation issue where tweet release tests depended on the `requests` module being installed.

### 5. Update renamed test reference in Makefile and CI contract
**Commit:** `a5a0fdd9`

Housekeeping fix to keep Makefile and CI contract aligned after test file renames.

---

## Refactoring / Internal Cleanup

- **Holistic cluster modules removed**: Several small accessor modules in `context_holistic/` were inlined (e.g., `_clusters_complexity.py`, `_clusters_consistency.py`, `_clusters_dependency.py`, `_clusters_security.py`, etc.) -- commit `99a8228a`
- **Rust compat cleanup**: Removed compat re-export wrapper in Rust custom detector, cleaned up fixers -- commit `99a8228a`
- **TypeScript dead code removal**: Deleted `helpers_blocks.py` and `helpers_line_state.py` from TS smell detectors (100 + 108 lines removed) -- commit `9dd5a430`
- **Issue semantics consolidation**: Large refactor of `issue_semantics.py` across commits `553174cc` and `f0b00123`, centralizing issue classification logic
- **Plan schema evolution**: Multiple incremental updates to `_plan/schema/__init__.py` across several commits
- **Work queue snapshot overhaul**: `snapshot.py` grew substantially (~473 line diff in `99a8228a`) with ranking refinements and phase-aware ordering
- **Reconcile module renames**: `reconcile.py` renamed to `scan_issue_reconcile.py` for clarity, `reconcile_review_import.py` refactored into `sync/review_import.py` -- commit `6bce1ccb`
- **Broad issue/state type updates**: `f0b00123` touched 131 files, mostly updating issue type references and state schema types across the entire codebase

---

## Community Contributions

1. **Andrew Robinson** (`c08b84de`): Fix to unblock objective resolves while triage is pending -- a meaningful UX improvement preventing workflow stalls
2. **Dragoy** (co-author on `7fe01b42`, PR #415): C/C++ detector scoping improvements -- security finding filtering, CMake test coverage mapping, header extension fixes

---

## Documentation

- **Commit `7ce7e283`**: Added `.desloppify/` gitignore reminder to README setup instructions (closes #416)

---

## Summary

The v0.9.9 release is primarily about **plan lifecycle robustness and workflow polish**. The headline features are: (1) auto-resolving issues for deleted files, (2) fixing a race condition in the reconciliation pipeline, (3) richer cluster semantics replacing string-sniffing heuristics, (4) more flexible triage attestation validation, and (5) C++ detector scoping improvements from a community contributor. There are also two important UX bug fixes -- unblocking resolves during pending triage (community fix) and better C++ first-party scoping.
